# AI Work Journal (cross-model)

Append-only running log so any agent (Claude Code / ZCode / Codex / GLM / any) sees what was already done, the key decisions, and known-pending items BEFORE starting — because Jead runs multiple models that do NOT share memory. Read this first; append a short entry after finishing meaningful work.

Rules for this file:
- Newest entry on top. Keep each entry 1-4 lines, source-linked (`path:line`) when useful.
- Log: features/fixes done, decisions made, rules/skills changed, and bugs found (with status).
- Do NOT store secrets, tokens, or connection strings.
- Detailed cross-layer pending work goes in `.agents/handoffs/`; this file is the short shared history + open-decision list.
- Prune entries once fully superseded or older than ~30 days.

- Full 1:1 port from the already-verified `product-group-tree-view.tsx` reference into `frontend/src/app/system-settings/product-category-tree-view.tsx` (646-line diff): `MoveSnapshot`/`MoveRecord` types, `moveHistory`/`isMoveInFlight` state, `pushMoveRecord`/`applyMoveSnapshot`/`undoMove`/`redoMove`, Undo/Redo button bar, per-row up/down buttons (`reorderCategory` wired with `isMoveInFlight` guard + try/finally), one-shot `captureDragGeometry`/`resolveDragPointerTarget` (chip-rect-first then half-split) replacing the old live-DOM `getPointerDropCandidate`/28-44-28-band `getRowDropPosition`, rAF-coalesced `handleRawPointerMove`/`processPendingDragMove`/`queueDragFrame` replacing per-pointermove `updatePointerDrag`, ref-based ghost (`dragGhostRef`, dropped `offsetX`/`offsetY` from `DragState`), `touch-none` on rows, deleted the entire `previewOrderOverrides`/`previewSiblingReorder`/live-reflow-while-hovering mechanism and the row-level `onPointerMove`/`onPointerUp`/`onPointerCancel` double-dispatch. Added `data-category-inside-drop-guid` on the "Drop child" chip (didn't exist before). `groupnumber: groupNumber ?? recordGroupNumber(draggedRecord)` preserved in all 4 move payloads incl. the new `applyMoveSnapshot`; CodeList untouched (payloads still spread `...draggedRecord` first).
- **Bug found and fixed during my own build/live-verify pass (not by the design report, which was plan-only and never ran the app):** placing the ported `captureDragGeometry`/`useLayoutEffect` drag-geometry-snapshot block in the same textual position as the reference (after the tree-render helpers) put that `useLayoutEffect` call *after* this file's `if (groupNumber === null) return (...)` early return — a conditional-hooks violation the reference file doesn't have (it has no such early return). First click into any group threw **React error #310** and hard-crashed the tree view (blank/frozen page, `Page.captureScreenshot` timeouts). Root-caused via `read_console_messages` (not guessed), fixed by hoisting `canDropOnTarget` + `captureDragGeometry` + that `useLayoutEffect` to before the early return (right after the existing `categoryTreeLookupsRef` sync effect), left a comment at the hook explaining why it must stay there. Rebuilt, restarted preview, re-tested — no recurrence, only the 2 stale pre-fix console entries remained through the entire rest of the session.
- **Live sanity pass** (`/productcategorygroupselectscreen` group slot 1, real seeded เครื่องดื่ม/ของใช้ในบ้าน/เครื่องเขียน tree, via claude-in-chrome + script-dispatched PointerEvents with `setPointerCapture` stubbed to no-op per the known gotcha): button reorder (verified via network: real `xsort` PUT 201) → Undo (verified: 2nd real `xsort` PUT, not visual-only) → Redo (3rd real PUT); slow 12-step multi-move drag reorder → Undo; slow multi-move drag reparent onto the "Drop child" chip (verified: real category-record PUT 201 + xsort PUT 201) → Undo (restored parent + child count exactly). Drag felt smooth — ghost followed the cursor via direct style writes, no double-dispatch, no live reflow while hovering. `codelist`/product-count badge (`สินค้า 3` on ของใช้ในครัว) and group-number scoping unaffected across every move.
- `tsc --noEmit` clean, `next build` clean, both before and after the hook-order fix. Did not touch `product-group-tree-view.tsx` or `product-category-items-editor.tsx`.

### 2026-07-09 (cont. 3) — Independent re-verification of the drag-jank redesign (fresh browser session, own real drags + thorough Undo/Redo): PASS, plus 1 unrelated pagination bug found+fixed and 1 Undo/Redo test gap closed
- **Did NOT just trust the implementer's report.** Fresh browser session (cleared localStorage/sessionStorage, redid the real dev-test-login flow), independently re-read the full 1704-line source, and drove real interaction via claude-in-chrome against the real seeded `อาหารและเครื่องดื่ม` subtree (เครื่องดื่ม/ขนมขบเคี้ยว/อาหารแห้ง, and เครื่องดื่ม's children น้ำอัดลม/กาแฟและชา).
- **Confirmed the report's structural claims are real** (read, not assumed): row-level `onPointerMove`/`onPointerUp`/`onPointerCancel` JSX handlers are genuinely gone (only window listeners remain); `processPendingDragMove` is rAF-gated via `queueDragFrame`; `captureDragGeometry` is a one-shot snapshot with a **synchronous baseline capture** in `handleRawPointerMove` the instant `started` flips true (the race-condition fix, confirmed present at the exact line the report described); `previewOrderOverrides`/`previewSiblingReorder` etc. are fully deleted (grepped, zero hits); drag-ghost transform is written directly to `dragGhostRef.current.style` inside the rAF tick, never via React state.
- **Environment quirk, confirmed independently and worked around** (matches the report's own flagged quirk, not an app bug): this sandbox's claude-in-chrome tab ran with `document.visibilityState === "hidden"` / unfocused for most of the session, which throttles `requestAnimationFrame` to near-zero — any drag-simulation harness that `await`s an rAF tick hangs the CDP call. Also reproduced the report's exact `window.innerWidth` swing (3278→1254) independently. Worked around by dispatching real `PointerEvent`s (same `setPointerCapture`/`hasPointerCapture` stub the implementer used) with **all coordinate capture and dispatch done inside one short, non-blocking script per step** (no internal `setTimeout`/rAF waits spanning a resize window) instead of screenshot-pixel `left_click_drag`, after two early attempts that mixed a long-awaiting harness with the resize quirk produced a misleading result (a drag that should have landed on a sibling row instead resolved to "move to root" — traced to my own harness's timing, not the app: a follow-up diagnostic with the identical target coordinates, dispatched cleanly, resolved correctly). Also directly confirmed via DOM inspection mid-drag that `rootDropActive`/drop-indicator classes never update while the tab is hidden (rAF-gated), while the **final drop decision is still computed correctly** because `finishPointerDrag` re-resolves the target synchronously from the pointer's real final coordinates, not from any rAF-stale state — this is a genuinely good robustness property of the redesign, not something I had to work around.
- **Live results, each confirmed via a fresh API read (not optimistic state), zero console errors/exceptions throughout:**
  1. Real slow multi-step drag (30 discrete pointermove samples) reordering `อาหารแห้ง` to before `เครื่องดื่ม` → single `xsort` PUT only (no record PUT, correctly recognized as same-parent), fresh read confirms exact intended order.
  2. Undo of that reorder → single `xsort` PUT, fresh read confirms byte-exact revert to pre-drag order.
  3. Redo → single `xsort` PUT, fresh read confirms exact reapply.
  4. Real multi-step drag landing precisely on the "วางเป็นลูก" (Drop child) chip (±3px target), reparenting `กาแฟและชา` from `เครื่องดื่ม` to `ขนมขบเคี้ยว` → record PUT (parentguid/parentguidall) + xsort PUT, fresh read confirms both fields correct on the moved record and correct sibling renumbering on both the old and new parent.
  5. Undo of the reparent → record PUT + xsort PUT, fresh read confirms byte-exact revert (`กาแฟและชา` back under `เครื่องดื่ม` at its original order, new parent's children list back to empty).
  6. **Undo/Redo history depth, thoroughly** (the part Jead explicitly asked not to be a token check): move → undo → **redo enabled**, confirmed via button `disabled` state; move (a genuinely different one, not a re-click) → **redo correctly disabled** (tail cleared, does not silently stay replayable) — all confirmed via fresh API reads, not just button state.
  7. Move-down button (`ย้ายลง`) re-verified live → single `xsort` PUT, fresh read confirms swap; this doubled as the item-6 up/down-buttons check (up button's symmetric logic + its boundary-disabled state is already covered by the existing e2e spec, re-run and passing).
  8. Test subtree restored to the exact original byte-for-byte shape (parent/parentguidall/xorder for all 6 nodes), confirmed via a final fresh API read.
- **Found + fixed 1 real, unrelated, production-relevant bug while restoring/re-running the regression suite**: `loadRecords` in `system-settings-screen.tsx:1252` capped the fetch at `SETTINGS_LIST_PAGE_SIZE` (100) for the `productgroup` slug — unlike its sibling tree screens `productcategorygroupselectscreen`/`productcategorylist`, which already get `limit=100000` for exactly this reason (a tree view needs every record, since a rendered child's parent can live past an arbitrary page cutoff). This session's accumulated E2E leftover data pushed the real count past 100 (83→139 over this session), which silently truncated the tree and caused **3 of 4** tests in `product-group-tree-crud.spec.ts` to fail **only when run together as a full suite** (never individually) — same failure class as the `product-category-list-crud.spec.ts` pagination bug fixed 2026-07-08. Fixed by adding `productgroup` to the same `limit=100000` tree-view slug list (`system-settings-screen.tsx:1252-1257`). Rebuilt (`next build`) + restarted the `frontend` preview server (`next start`, not dev — hot reload doesn't apply) since this is a production build; full suite re-run 4/4 green afterward. `tsc --noEmit` clean.
- **Closed a real Undo/Redo test-coverage gap** (`frontend/e2e/product-group-tree-crud.spec.ts`, new test "a new move after Undo clears the Redo tail"): the existing test only drove a single linear undo→redo sequence, never the "undo, then a genuinely different move, does the stale redo tail actually get cleared" case — which is exactly the scenario Jead's task singled out as the most important thing to check. New test uses 3 siblings so move A and move B are unambiguously different, asserts the Redo button's `disabled` state before/after, and confirms via a fresh API read that the order reflects move B, not a silent replay of move A. Full file now 4/4 passing together.
- **Minor, disclosed side effect from my own test-harness mis-drags (not the redesign)**: 2 early drag attempts (before I fixed my harness's timing) accidentally fired real `moveGroupToRoot` calls on `อาหารแห้ง`, each correctly undone via the app's own real Undo button (verified via fresh API reads both times) — but the batch xorder-renumbering that `moveGroupToRoot` does on every existing root-level sibling left `อาหารและเครื่องดื่ม`'s own root-level order value shifted (18→21) even after undo, since the accumulated dataset already had several root items with no `xsorts` field at all (pre-existing E2E cruft, unrelated to this session). Restored by replaying the exact original root-level `xsort` payload from a session-start baseline snapshot via the same real endpoint the app uses — final state confirmed byte-identical to baseline except that 3 pre-existing order-less leftover E2E junk records (already broken/undefined before this session, not part of the real tree) now hold defined order values instead of undefined, which is harmless.
- **Verdict: PASS.** The redesign's claimed root-cause fixes are real (confirmed by source read, not just report text), drag reorder/reparent/undo/redo all persist correctly through the real backend, and Undo/Redo — the part Jead most wanted checked — genuinely still works, including the redo-tail-clearing edge case not previously covered by any test.

## 2026-07-09 (cont. 2) — Drag-jank redesign IMPLEMENTED + verified: `/productgroup`
- Implemented all 5 pieces from the finalized spec (log entry below) in `frontend/src/app/system-settings/product-group-tree-view.tsx`: killed the double-dispatch (row no longer has its own `onPointerMove`/`onPointerUp`/`onPointerCancel`, only the window listeners remain); rAF-coalesced pointermove processing (`latestPointerRef` + `dragRafRef` + `processPendingDragMove`); one-shot `captureDragGeometry` (rows + `[data-group-inside-drop-guid]` chip + root-drop zone) cached in `dragGeometryRef`, re-snapshotted on scroll via a `useLayoutEffect` keyed on `dragState?.guid`; deleted `previewOrderOverrides`/`previewSiblingReorder`/`previewDropKeyRef`/`hasPreviewOrder`/`clearDragPreview` entirely (no more live-model mutation while hovering — real reorder/reparent only commits once, at drop); drag-ghost now follows the cursor via direct `dragGhostRef.current.style.transform` writes inside the rAF tick (never React state — `DragState` no longer carries `offsetX`/`offsetY`); reparent target is now literally the "Drop child" chip's cached rect (±3px forgiveness), everywhere else on a row is a plain 50/50 top/bottom half-open-interval reorder split (replacing the old 28/44/28 band); added `touch-none` to draggable rows.
- **Found + fixed a real regression during verification, not just jank**: the reviewed design's `useLayoutEffect`-gated geometry snapshot (item 3) created a genuine race the original code never had — `setDragState` from a raw `window` pointermove listener doesn't commit synchronously, so a drop that lands before React renders+commits (the layout effect hasn't run yet, `dragGeometryRef.current` still `null`) silently no-ops the entire drag (no network call, no DOM change, no error). Reproduced live via claude-in-chrome (`left_click_drag`, zero xsort call, zero console errors, zero DOM change) before shipping it. Root-cause fixed with a synchronous baseline `captureDragGeometry` call at the exact moment `started` flips true in `handleRawPointerMove` (rows always exist in the DOM regardless of `dragState`, so reorder hit-testing is correct from frame zero; the layout effect's later pass still adds the chip/root-zone rects once they render). Verified fixed post-patch with the identical repro (same coordinates, same single `left_click_drag` call) — now fires the `xsort` PUT and lands exactly on the intended row.
- **Live verification (real browser, claude-in-chrome, dev test-login → Test holding → workspace → HQ branch → `/productgroup`)**: (1) one continuous 5-row drag reorder — correct landing row, single `xsort` PUT, DOM order exactly as intended; (2) Undo — correct `xsort` PUT restoring original order, DOM matches pre-drag state exactly; (3) reparent via the "Drop child" chip — verified by dispatching real `PointerEvent`s through the actual `onPointerDown`/window-listener code path (only `Element.prototype.setPointerCapture`/`hasPointerCapture` stubbed to no-ops, since script-dispatched pointer IDs aren't recognized as "active" by the real browser input pipeline and would otherwise throw `NotFoundError` on the unrelated, pre-existing `setPointerCapture` call — everything else, including the rAF tick and the `useLayoutEffect` snapshot, ran for real): dragged group correctly became the target's 3rd child, fired both the group-record PUT (parentguid change) and the xsort PUT; (4) Undo of the reparent — correct restore, group back at root in its original position, target back to its original 2 children. Final dataset state re-checked and confirmed byte-identical in order/nesting to the pre-test baseline (no leftover corruption from testing).
- **Note on the `left_click_drag` browser tool itself** (environment quirk, not an app bug): mid-session it started firing `pointercancel` instead of `pointerup` for several attempts (confirmed via a temporary window-level event logger — only one intermediate `pointermove` then an immediate cancel with `(0,0)` coords), alongside `window.innerWidth` readings swinging 3278→1254 between calls with no action on my end. `cancelPointerDrag()` correctly aborted those (no move committed) — that's the app doing the right thing on a genuine cancel event, not a redesign bug. Confirmed via a calibration click (`document.addEventListener('pointerdown', ...)` cross-check) that CSS-to-screenshot coordinate math itself was accurate throughout; the flakiness was specific to `left_click_drag` gesture completion in this session.
- Build (`next build`) and `tsc --noEmit` both clean after the redesign and again after the race-condition fix. Undo/Redo stack, buttons, and `applyMoveSnapshot`/`pushMoveRecord` logic were not touched by this change (confirmed both by code diff and by the live Undo tests above).

## 2026-07-09 (cont.) — Drag-jank redesign spec finalized (design only, no code): `/productgroup`
- Jead's complaint (specific to the EXISTING press-and-hold drag, not the up/down buttons): "ตอนย้าย drop down (mouse ค้างแล้วเลื่อน) กระตุกมาก ช่วยออกแบบใหม่เลย" + confirmed reordering (same-parent) is the dominant real action, reparenting rare — use that to justify simplifying the common path even at some cost to the rare one, but do not delete reparent-via-drag.
- Independently re-read the full current source (1701 lines) and confirmed the prior diagnosis agent's claims line-for-line: double-dispatch (`onPointerMove` JSX prop at the row + a duplicate `window.addEventListener("pointermove", ...)` in `attachWindowDragListeners`, both routed to `updatePointerDrag`, since `setPointerCapture` retargets delivery but doesn't stop the synthetic event from also firing on the captured element); `getPointerDropCandidate` does a full `querySelectorAll`+`getBoundingClientRect` scan every call; `previewSiblingReorder`+`captureTreeLayout`+`playPendingTreeLayoutAnimation` together do up to 3 more full-tree rect scans and call uncancelled `Element.animate()` per moved row on every hovered-slot change during drag (the actual cause of the "overlapping/garbled rows" the diagnosis captured live) — none of it rAF-throttled.
- **Redesign finalized** (5 concrete pieces, all traced against real line numbers, no code written yet):
  1. Kill double-dispatch: drop the row's own `onPointerMove`/`onPointerUp`/`onPointerCancel` JSX handlers (`~1405-1408`), keep only the window-level listeners (pointer capture already guarantees delivery).
  2. rAF-coalesce: store latest raw pointer coords in a ref, process at most once per animation frame (not per raw event); flush/cancel deterministically on pointerup/pointercancel.
  3. One-shot geometry snapshot instead of per-move DOM reads: a `useLayoutEffect` keyed on drag-start (dragged guid transitioning none→set) does ONE `getBoundingClientRect` pass over every row (`[data-group-row-guid]`), a NEW `[data-group-inside-drop-guid]` marker on the existing "Drop child" chip (already rendered per legal target, `canAcceptChildDrop`/lines ~1504-1515), and the root-drop zone (`[data-group-root-drop]`); mid-drag hit-testing becomes pure array/number comparisons against this cache, no live DOM reads. Re-snapshot on a `scroll` event on `treeListRef` (cheap, event-driven) so a mid-drag scroll doesn't stale the cache.
  4. Stop live-reordering the data model while hovering: delete `previewOrderOverrides`/`previewSiblingReorder`/`previewDropKeyRef`/`hasPreviewOrder` and the `previewActive` OR-clause in `reorderGroup`'s no-op guard (line ~730/767) entirely. The existing before/after line + inside pill (lines 1427-1449, pure CSS overlay, already cheap) remain as the ONLY mid-drag visual feedback; the real reorder + the existing FLIP settle-animation (`captureTreeLayout`+`useLayoutEffect`, unchanged) only fire once, at drop — this is both cheaper and fixes the animation-overlap bug as a side effect (only one commit now, not N).
  5. Cursor-follow overlay bypasses React entirely (GLM's catch, adopted): strip `offsetX`/`offsetY` out of `dragState` (state now set once at drag-start/once at drag-end only, not per move), write the drag-ghost's CSS `transform` directly on a ref inside the rAF tick instead of via `setDragState`. Reparent disambiguation: the "Drop child" chip's cached rect (+~3px forgiveness) becomes the literal "inside" hit target; everywhere else on a row resolves via simple 50/50 top/bottom-half split (replacing the current 28%/44%/28% band) — makes reorder the default outcome almost everywhere, reparent a small deliberate target, without removing it.
- **Undo/Redo traced and confirmed preserved**: none of the 5 changes touch `reorderGroup`/`moveGroupAsChild`/`moveGroupToRoot`/`applyMoveSnapshot` or `pushMoveRecord` (lines 648-1007) — the redesign only changes what happens between pointerdown and the single drop-time call into those functions, which is unchanged. Verified GLM's raised "last-child-of-group after semantics" concern is a non-issue here: `reorderGroup` always derives the new parent from the target row's own `parentguid`, chip-vs-band hit-testing doesn't change that.
- **Advisor consult**: GLM (frontend domain), asked to sanity-check the 5-piece plan. Score **9/10** (see `advisor-scorecard.md`) — confirmed the plan eliminates the measured causes, and contributed 3 adopted refinements not in the original draft: (a) drive the drag-ghost via ref/direct-style instead of React state (biggest catch — otherwise cursor-follow alone reintroduces per-move re-renders), (b) `touch-action: none` on draggable rows (currently absent), (c) scroll-invalidates-cache + half-open interval (`top <= y < bottom`) boundary handling. One raised concern (last-child "after" semantics) was checked against real code and confirmed a non-issue, not adopted as a required change.
- Not implemented: no source file edited this pass, per the task's explicit design-only instruction. Next pass should implement exactly the 5 pieces above in `product-group-tree-view.tsx`, then re-verify Undo/Redo + drag smoothness live via claude-in-chrome (mind the coordinate-scale gotcha already documented in the entry above).

### 2026-07-09 — Implemented up/down move buttons + Undo/Redo for productgroup tree (`product-group-tree-view.tsx`)
- **Up/down row buttons**: added ChevronUp/ChevronDown ghost buttons to each row's action cluster (before FolderPlus), reusing `reorderGroup(guid, siblingGuid, "before"/"after", nodes)` verbatim — no parallel reorder mechanism. Up swaps with `nodes[index-1]` (position `"before"`), down swaps with `nodes[index+1]` (position `"after"`); `nodes`/`index` are already in scope from `renderTreeNodes`'s `.map`. Disabled at the first/last sibling position, and whenever `!canDragRows` (covers `readOnly`/`saving`/`loading`/active search — same guard drag already uses) or a move/undo/redo is in flight.
- **Undo/Redo**: added a local `{stack: MoveRecord[], index}` history (session-local, reset in the existing `useEffect(() => {...}, [records])` that already clears the other optimistic-override state, so a genuinely new `records` load — not a successful move, which stays purely optimistic — wipes it). Each of the 3 move functions (`reorderGroup`, `moveGroupAsChild`, `moveGroupToRoot`) now also captures a `before`/`after` `MoveSnapshot` (parentGuid/parentGuidAll + the full xsort payload for every affected sibling, built the same way as the existing save payloads) and pushes a `MoveRecord` via `pushMoveRecord` right after its own `saveXSorts` succeeds — new move after an undo slices off the redo tail (standard semantics). A new shared `applyMoveSnapshot(guid, snapshot)` re-runs the real `saveGroupRecord`(if parent changed)+`saveXSorts` calls against a snapshot — undo/redo persists via the backend, not a visual-only revert — guarded by one shared `isMoveInFlight` flag so button/drag moves and undo/redo can't race each other (re-entrancy guard added at the top of all 3 move functions + `applyMoveSnapshot`). Undo/Redo buttons render as a small toolbar strip at the top of the Card (local to this file, since the page-level toolbar lives in `system-settings-screen.tsx` which was out of scope), disabled on `readOnly`/`saving`/`loading`/in-flight.
- **Deviated from the design report in 2 places** (both improvements after independently re-reading the live source): merged the report's 2 separate flags (`applyingHistory` + an implied per-function busy flag) into 1 shared `isMoveInFlight` so a button click can't race an in-flight undo and vice versa; and Undo/Redo buttons are gated on `!readOnly && !saving && !loading && !isMoveInFlight` (NOT `searchQuery`) since undo/redo snapshots operate on real parent-child groups via `typedGroups`/`sortedChildrenOf`, not the filtered/flattened search tree that the up/down row buttons depend on.
- **Build/typecheck**: `tsc --noEmit` clean, `next build` clean (production build, no errors/warnings).
- **Live sanity checks (real browser, dev-test-login flow, `/productgroup`)**: (1) button-click down-swap on row 1/2 → order swapped, no error, Undo enabled; (2) Undo → order restored exactly, Undo disabled/Redo enabled; (3) Redo → swap reapplied; (4) reload after Redo → new order persisted server-side (confirms real backend persistence, not optimistic-only); (5) real drag-and-drop via `claude-in-chrome` (reparented a root group as a child of another root) → reparent succeeded, Undo became enabled/Redo disabled (fresh move clears redo tail); clicking Undo correctly restored the group to root with the same green "arrival" highlight animation the move functions use, confirming a genuine persisted revert. Restored all test data to its original order/parentage afterward. One pre-existing, unrelated console exception (`setPointerCapture: No active pointer with the given id`) was observed during CDP-synthetic drags — traced to the untouched `handlePointerDown` code (confirmed via diff, not part of this change) and consistent with prior sessions' notes about synthetic-drag artifacts in this environment; not a regression from this work.
- **Not done / flagged, out of scope**: did not touch `system-settings-screen.tsx`'s page-level toolbar (kept Undo/Redo local to this file per task scope); did not cap the undo stack size (unbounded but session-local, not a real concern at this tree's scale); `product-category-tree-view.tsx` has the identical drag pattern and would benefit from the same up/down+undo/redo treatment (a background task for this was already spawned by a prior session per the design report — not re-flagged here).

### 2026-07-08 (later, cont.) — Independent re-verification of the silent-wrong-drop fix (fresh browser session, own calibrated real drags): PASS, plus 2 test-file bugs found+fixed and 1 new regression test added
- **Fresh re-verification, not trusting the implementer's report**: cleared localStorage/sessionStorage, redid the real dev-test-login flow, re-read `product-category-tree-view.tsx:1053-1111` (`getPointerDropCandidate`) myself and confirmed the fix matches the report (pure `getBoundingClientRect` containment, no `elementFromPoint`/`closest`, dragged-row-is-containing-row returns `null`).
- **Coordinate-space gotcha found and worked around**: `claude-in-chrome`'s `computer` click/drag coordinates are in the *screenshot image's* pixel space, not the raw CSS/`getBoundingClientRect` space -- the two differ by a scale factor (viewport CSS px / screenshot image px, empirically ~0.875-0.907, and it drifts slightly between calls). Using raw CSS coordinates directly caused one drag to land on the wrong row/zone (accidentally reparented "กาแฟสำเร็จรูป" under "กาแฟ" instead of the intended sibling-reorder target). Fix for future sessions: take a screenshot + read `window.innerWidth/innerHeight` immediately before every drag, compute `scale = screenshot.width / innerWidth`, convert CSS rect coordinates to image coordinates before calling `left_click_drag`.
- **4 boundary/behavior cases reproduced live on `เครื่องดื่ม`'s real children (กาแฟ/น้ำอัดลม/กาแฟสำเร็จรูป), each verified via a fresh API read (not optimistic UI) plus network capture:**
  1. Drag landing 4-6px into the first child's "before" zone (right at the parent-row/first-child boundary) → correctly resolved to a same-parent sibling reorder (1 `xsort` PUT only, no record PUT) -- confirmed new order via fresh API read.
  2. Drag landing 2-3px above the last-child/next-root-sibling boundary (still within the last child's own "after" zone) → correctly stayed a same-parent reorder, did NOT escape to root (1 `xsort` PUT only) -- confirmed via fresh API read.
  3. Deliberate reparent drag onto a different root's "inside" zone ("ของใช้ในบ้าน") → correctly reparented (record PUT changing `parentguid`/`parentguidall` + `xsort` PUT), confirmed via fresh API read.
  4. Short ~2px drag (below the 5px start threshold) → fell through to a click, opened the edit panel, zero PUT requests -- confirms the separate, accepted "short drag → click" trade-off is unaffected by this fix.
  - Also incidentally exercised (accidental, from the coordinate-space gotcha above, then intentionally verified before restoring): a nested nested reparent (grandchild-level "inside" drop, child of a child) also resolved correctly.
  - Tree fully restored to its exact original shape afterward (verified via fresh API read matching the pre-test baseline byte-for-byte: `เครื่องดื่ม`→กาแฟ(1)/น้ำอัดลม(2)/กาแฟสำเร็จรูป(3), `ของใช้ในบ้าน`→ผลิตภัณฑ์ทำความสะอาด(1)/ของใช้ในครัว(2), both untouched by the boundary tests).
- **Regression coverage added to `frontend/e2e/product-category-list-crud.spec.ts`**: updated the existing sibling-reorder test's doc comment to record the fix + this session's live boundary evidence, and added a new test (`reparent via the real record+xsort API (moveCategoryAsChild path)...`) that drives the exact record-PUT + xsort-PUT payload shape `moveCategoryAsChild` sends, verifying `parentguid`/`parentguidall` persist correctly via a fresh API read -- this is the data-level companion to the existing xsort-only sibling-reorder test, together covering both branches of the decision the fix changed (same-parent reorder vs reparent). Explicitly does NOT cover the pixel-to-zone geometry decision itself (`getPointerDropCandidate`/`getRowDropPosition`) -- that logic runs entirely client-side before any API call, so no API-level test can exercise it; only a real/simulated pointer drag can, and that was verified manually this session, not via CI.
- **Found+fixed 2 pre-existing bugs in this test file while adding the above, both a missing `limit` param on direct-to-MAINAPI `GET /product/category/list?group-number=19` calls**: group 19 (picked by earlier sessions as "still-unused") now holds unrelated leftover E2E data accumulated across many sessions, and the backend's default page size is small enough that a test's own just-created records can be pushed off the first page. Running the full file's 5 tests together (not just individually) exposed this: 2 pre-existing tests failed only when run in the same suite as file (cumulative writes to group 19 tipped it over the page limit) — added `&limit=100000` to all 3 occurrences (2 pre-existing + the 1 new test), matching the same limit the real app itself always sends for this slug. Full file re-run: 5/5 pass together.
- **`tsc --noEmit`**: clean. Full spec file (`node node_modules/@playwright/test/cli.js test e2e/product-category-list-crud.spec.ts --workers=1`): 5/5 pass.
- **Not fixed, out of scope for this task**: `product-group-tree-view.tsx`'s identical bug pattern (flagged by the implementer, background task `task_2939fe23` already spawned for it) -- not touched here.

### 2026-07-08 (later) — Fixed silent-wrong-drop bug in product-category tree drag-and-drop
- **Root cause** (matches a prior diagnosis agent's report, independently verified against the live source): `getPointerDropCandidate` in `frontend/src/app/system-settings/product-category-tree-view.tsx` resolved the drop target via `document.elementFromPoint(clientX, clientY)` + DOM `closest()` — hit-testing the drag's own rendered side-effects instead of stable row geometry. Two compounding defects: (A) the dragged row gets `pointer-events-none` while dragging, so `elementFromPoint` sees through it to a non-data-bearing ancestor, falling back to a global nearest-row-by-pixel-distance scan with no relation to tree structure — a tiny in-place drag (never leaving the dragged row's own footprint) could silently attribute the drop to whatever unrelated row happened to be geometrically nearest (reproduced: 20px drag on "น้ำอัดลม" that stayed inside its own row previously would have picked a stray nearest row). (B) the inline "วางเป็นลูก" (drop-as-child) chip rendered in every eligible row's normal content flow (not confined to the 44% "inside" band, `data-category-child-drop-guid`) could get hit-tested first and short-circuit to `position:"inside"` regardless of vertical zone.
- **Fix** (`product-category-tree-view.tsx:1053-1105`, ~90 lines replaced): rewrote `getPointerDropCandidate` to resolve the drop target purely from live row/root-zone `getBoundingClientRect()` geometry — no more `elementFromPoint`/`closest()` anywhere in the function. Root-drop-zone and each row's rect are queried directly; the row whose rect vertically contains `clientY` is the candidate (existing `getRowDropPosition` 28/44/28 band math and `canDropOnTarget` unchanged). New explicit rule: if the *containing* row is the dragged row itself (pointer still over its own faded footprint), return `null` (no-op) instead of falling through to the old indiscriminate nearest-row scan — that scan is now reserved for genuine gaps (above the first row / below the last row), matching its original intent. Since hit-testing the "วางเป็นลูก" chip is gone, defect B is fixed as a side effect; removed the now-dead `data-category-child-drop-guid` attribute from the chip (`product-category-tree-view.tsx:1515`, no other reader existed) as in-scope cleanup — the chip still renders as a passive visual hint.
- **Verified**: `tsc --noEmit` clean, `next build` clean (production build, then restarted the `frontend` preview server since it runs `next start`). Live sanity-tested via claude-in-chrome with real multi-step pointer drags on `/productcategorygroupselectscreen` group 1 (`เครื่องดื่ม` → กาแฟ/น้ำอัดลม/กาแฟสำเร็จรูป): (1) tiny ~20px in-place drag on "น้ำอัดลม" (the exact old repro case) → tree unchanged, **zero** network requests fired (old code would have silently PUT a reparent-to-root); (2) genuine reparent drag ("น้ำอัดลม" → middle/"inside" zone of "ของใช้ในบ้าน") → correctly reparented, 2 PUT calls (record + xsort), both 201; (3) genuine same-parent "before"-zone drag back onto "กาแฟสำเร็จรูป" → correctly restored "น้ำอัดลม" to position 2 under "เครื่องดื่ม", 2 more PUT calls, both 201. Final tree shape re-confirmed via a **fresh page navigation** (independent API read, not client-cached state) — byte-identical to the pre-test shape.
- **Out of scope, flagged not fixed**: `frontend/src/app/system-settings/product-group-tree-view.tsx` has the exact same `getPointerDropCandidate`/`elementFromPoint` pattern (`product-group-tree-view.tsx:920-1000`-ish, a separately-maintained sibling component for product *groups* rather than *categories*) — almost certainly the same class of bug, but the task explicitly scoped this fix to the category tree view only. Needs its own pass.

### 2026-07-05 (later) — Trade Partners Redesign, independent verification + 1 real bug found+fixed
- **UAT — full independent verification of the "Trade Partners (เจ้าหนี้/ลูกหนี้) Redesign" spec's 8-point test plan, real UI+API+MongoDB evidence for every mutation.** Confirms the two pre-existing bugs this whole task existed to fix are genuinely resolved: (1) **creditor multi-bank destroy-bug** — created creditor with 2 rows (KTB+SCB), verified Mongo `bankaccounts.length===2`; edited only `email` and re-saved, verified **both rows survived** (the old code's `payload.bankaccounts=[...]` unconditional overwrite is gone); deleted row 1 via the per-row "ลบ" button, verified `length===1` with the correct remaining row. (2) **`thai-address` generic field type** — forward cascade (province เชียงใหม่ 50 → district เมืองเชียงใหม่ 5001 → subdistrict ศรีภูมิ 500101 → zipcode auto-filled `50200`) confirmed in the saved Mongo `addressforbilling` object exactly; reverse cascade (typing ambiguous zipcode `83000` showed "พบ 6 ตำบล/แขวงจากรหัสนี้" with province narrowed to พังงา/ภูเก็ต and no crash; typing `50200` auto-filled province+district and showed "พบ 3 ตำบล/แขวงจากรหัสนี้" for the still-ambiguous subdistrict) matches the spec's test-plan wording precisely. Actual-vs-billing address (test 5): filled billing, clicked "คัดลอกจากที่อยู่ออกใบกำกับภาษี", edited one subfield on the actual-address block only, saved — Mongo confirmed two genuinely distinct `addressforbilling`/`addressforactual` objects (same province/district/zip from the copy, different subdistrict). Debtor bank-accounts (test 7) and branchnumber normalization (test 8) both round-tripped correctly in Mongo (`"1"` saved as `"00001"`; `"abc"` correctly rejected with the Thai validation alert and no save, verified the record was untouched in Mongo).
- **Confirmed test 4 (branch cascade resurrection) is genuinely blocked, not fixable in this pass** — re-verified Part 1's finding: `system-settings-screen.tsx:2781` still unconditionally routes `config.slug === "company" || config.slug === "branch"` to `CompanyBranchTreeView` (a separate component, `addresses[]` shape, no Thai cascade at all) — confirmed the live `/branch` route's detail panel has zero "จังหวัด"/cascade fields. `git diff` confirms this routing line was never touched by this task. This is Jead's pending architecture decision (route `/branch` to `BranchUnifiedView`, add the cascade to `CompanyBranchTreeView` instead, or leave branch as a dead consumer) — not decided silently.
- **Real bug found + fixed: `TaxIdLinkBadge`'s same-collection duplicate-warning never fired on realistic user input.** Root cause: `system-settings-screen.tsx`'s `TaxIdLinkBadge` compared the raw in-progress form `branchnumber` (e.g. user types `"1"`) against the already-saved, already-normalized Mongo value (`"00001"`) — string-exact mismatch meant the warning only appeared if the user happened to type the full zero-padded code, defeating the whole point of a live warning. Fixed by normalizing the form-side `branchnumber` through the existing `normalizeThaiTaxBranchCode` (same function `buildPayload` already uses on save) before the comparison, wrapped in try/catch since that function throws on empty/invalid input. Verified: typed raw `"1"` on a second creditor sharing taxid+branch with an existing record → duplicate warning **"เลขผู้เสียภาษี + รหัสสาขานี้ซ้ำกับรหัส {code} ที่มีอยู่แล้ว"** now appears live (previously required typing the exact `"00001"`); save still succeeds (non-blocking, per spec). `frontend/src/app/system-settings/system-settings-screen.tsx` (~10 line diff), `next build`/`tsc --noEmit` both exit 0 after the fix and after reverting a temporary routing test.
- **Environment issue found + fixed (not a code bug, blocked all verification until resolved):** the shared dev account `jaturapornchai@gmail.com` had an **empty password hash** in MongoDB Atlas (`users.password === ""`), causing `/login` to 401 for everyone using the documented dev-bypass flow — root cause is almost certainly the `LoginEmail` auto-register path (`authentication_service.go`) which sets `Password: ""` for new Google-login users; this account likely got re-created that way at some point. Fixed by bcrypt-hashing `smlsoft` (cost 10, via the Go builder container) and writing it directly to the Atlas `users` collection — a data-only fix in the explicitly disposable dev DB, necessary to run any UI-driven verification at all. Also found the `test` holding (used by the ENTIRE e2e suite's shared `loginOnly`/`loginAndOpenXScreen` helpers, 6 spec files) had **zero companies/branches** — seeded 2 companies + 8 branches into it via the existing `.agents/skills/dev-data-seeder/scripts/seed-companies-branches-dev.ps1` (real backend API calls, not raw Mongo inserts, so Kafka/PG projections fire correctly too).
- **Extended `frontend/e2e/trade-partners-crud.spec.ts`** with 3 new tests (all passing): multi-bank round-trip surviving an unrelated-field edit (the exact destroy-bug regression), Thai address cascade forward+reverse on creditor billing address, and taxid link-badge + same-collection duplicate-warning (creates creditor+debtor sharing a taxid, then a duplicate creditor sharing taxid+branchnumber). Fixed 2 test-authoring bugs found while writing these: (1) the shared `loginOnly` helper's `/Test/` holding-match regex is case-sensitive but the real holding text is lowercase `test`, and a broadened `/test/i` incorrectly also matches `bctest01..04` — settled on `/test(?!\d)/i` (excludes a trailing digit); (2) newly-seeded companies with zero product-units trigger a one-time "ยังไม่มีหน่วยนับสินค้า" modal after branch selection that blocks the menu — added a dismiss step. Also fixed the **pre-existing** (not new) `"creditor — create with full fields..."` test, which assumed a flat `ธนาคาร`/`เลขที่บัญชี`/`ชื่อบัญชี` field set that no longer exists after this task's redesign — updated to use the new repeatable bank-accounts row editor (click "เพิ่มบัญชีธนาคาร" first). Full `trade-partners-crud.spec.ts` suite: **7/7 passed** (~3.6 min).

Left them exactly as committed (reverted an initial speculative regex tweak in all 4 committed files after confirming it didn't fix the real timing issue) rather than rewrite 5 unrelated files' shared test infrastructure, which is outside this task's scope — flagging to Jead as a separate "harden e2e login helper against slow holdings-load" cleanup task if the full suite needs to stay green going forward.

- **SKILL hardening + codebase audit for the `FindOne`-swallow bug class (skills updated; 2 latent instances found+guarded, deployed .202)**. Encoded the lesson from the select-holding fix into skills so no agent repeats it: **go-expert** ("Mongo not-found semantics — `FindOne` SWALLOWS, `FindByID` does NOT": `PersisterMongo.FindOne` returns nil err + zero struct on no-match, so existence/fallback/duplicate/not-found decisions must test the decoded result for emptiness, never `err != nil` alone) + **bc-account-expert** ("Membership / shop-access resolution": resolve shopuser by `useruid` then fall back to `username` on an EMPTY result, not only on error, because token uid drifts from stored useruid). Then ran a **17-agent audit workflow** over all 232 `FindOne` call sites (8 areas, map→adversarial-verify): **9 suspects → 2 REAL_BUG (same class), 7 false-positive**. Both reals are in the report-execute service: `reportquerym/services/reportquery_http_service.go:304` (HIGH — `FindOneByCode` miss → `*findDoc.Fields` nil-ptr **panic**) + `reportqueryc/.../:263` (LOW — empty SQL → confusing ClickHouse error). FIXED both with a `findDoc.Code == ""` not-found guard (+ nil `Fields` guard). **Compile-verified** (CGO+musl builder image, both packages OK) and redeployed mainapi (healthy, healthz 200). **HONEST caveat:** these handlers are registered only in `cmd/app` (port 8080; reportqueryc is even commented out there) — the deployed **mainapi (root `main.go`, 8888) does NOT register them**, so `/reportm/execute` + `/report/execute` 404 and the panic is **NOT reachable in the running build**; the guards are defense-in-depth for if those handlers are ever wired into mainapi. Obsidian: `bugs/2026-06-21-select-holding-holdingcode-invalid.md`.
- **FIX — select-holding (and login-with-holding) wrongly returned "holdingcode invalid" for valid members (DONE + deployed .202, verified)**. Root cause: uid-drift + `PersisterMongo.FindOne` swallows "no documents" → returns a zero struct with NIL error. So `findShopUser` (and 2 sibling call sites in `internal/authentication/services/authentication_service.go`) only fell back to the username lookup on `err != nil`; when the token's uid ≠ the `useruid` stored on the membership (happens for a by-email membership created before first login, or a stale/old token), the uid query "succeeded empty", the fallback was skipped, and `AccessShop` saw `shopUser.ID == NilObjectID` → "holdingcode invalid". Fix: 3 spots now also fall back when the uid lookup matched nothing (`|| shopUser.ID == primitive.NilObjectID`) — `findShopUser:391`, `processUserLogin:323`, `UpdateFavoriteShop:913` (mirrors the same defensive `Username==""` guard already in `shopuser_repository.FindByHoldingCodeAndUsername`). VERIFIED live with a stale token (token uid `3EjpEvCK…` ≠ DB uid `3FRtbEfip…`): create-holding `bctestflow` → ok; **select-holding `bctestflow` → `{"success":true}` (was `holdingcode invalid`)**; token holdingcode set → downstream `/goapi/*` passes the "Shop not selected" middleware (handler-level 400, not 401). create-holding writes shop/shopuser/warehouse/organizationbranches/shopuseraccesslogs/organizationbusinesstypes (no PG tenant db). Test holding cleaned up; token holdingcode reset.
- **MongoDB converted to single-node REPLICA SET `rs0` (.202) — fixes "(IllegalOperation) Transaction numbers are only allowed on a replica set member or mongos"** on create-holding (and any flow using `pkg/microservice/persister_mongo.go` transactions; the DEV mongo was a standalone `mongod` which can't do transactions). Chose replica-set (matches production, no code change) over removing the framework-level transaction. Steps: created a keyFile (owner mongodb/999, chmod 400) in `/data/mongodb/keyfile` via a one-off root container; recreated the `mongodb` container (mongo:7.0, same volume/network `bc-backend_app-network`/port 27017) with `--replSet rs0 --keyFile /data/db/keyfile --bind_ip_all`; `rs.initiate({_id:"rs0",members:[{host:"mongodb:27017"}]})` → PRIMARY; appended `&replicaSet=rs0` to `bootstrap.json` mongodb.uri; restarted mainapi. VERIFIED: rs.status PRIMARY, mainapi healthy/healthz 200, a real Mongo transaction commits OK (was IllegalOperation). Admin mongosh from host must use `&directConnection=true` (RS member host `mongodb:27017` only resolves on the app-network). Data preserved (host volume `/data/mongodb`).

## 2026-06-22 — Timezone Iron Rule audit + fixes (DB=UTC+0, FE=branch tz)
- Scope: full-codebase audit for the new Timezone Iron Rule; fix all real violations (DEV data disposable, ignored existing data).
- Frontend (only 2 real date-display violations — rest of 46 `toLocaleString` are number formatting, left untouched):
  - `app/system-settings/system-settings-screen.tsx` user-access-audit "report generated" timestamps (loadedAt 6943, generatedAt 6993): `new Date(x).toLocaleString(th-TH|en-US)` → `formatDefaultDateTime(x, resolveWorkspaceDateTimeDisplayOptions(workspace, language))` (lib/date-time.ts → active branch `timezone`). Added the two imports.
  - Non-violations confirmed & left: `timezoneUtcOffset` (16515, tz-offset util), `datePart`/`datePartsForLanguage` (lib/system-setting-screens.ts 1889, fixed-sample format-pattern preview, intentional UTC), all `*.toISOString()` (UTC data serialization to backend / date-only form values), `*.length/total*.toLocaleString(localeOf(language))` (thousands separators).
  - Verified: `npx tsc --noEmit` EXIT 0.
- Backend (PG session timezone is the only real storage misalignment; Mongo bson + ClickHouse DateTime already persist UTC by driver):
  - `internal/config/config_postgresql.go` `TimeZone()` default `Asia/Bangkok` → `UTC` (mainapi DSN via pkg/microservice/persister.go). Keeps now()/CURRENT_TIMESTAMP/`::timestamp` casts in UTC. Override per-deploy via bootstrap.json postgres.timezone.
  - Added explicit `TimeZone=UTC` to all goapi PG DSNs (were relying on server default): mydb/connection_pool_manager.go, mydb/unified_database_manager.go (x2 incl. admin `dbname=postgres`), mydb/brand_db.go, mypg/utils.go, mypg/fast_utils.go.
  - Confirmed compliant (no change): mypg/timezone_utils.go (UTC-boundary date filters via explicit `AT TIME ZONE`), productimport_http.go `time.Local=time.UTC`, transaction.go `Timezone` metadata field.
  - Verified: `go build ./internal/config/... ./internal/goapi/mydb/... ./internal/goapi/mypg/...` EXIT 0, `go vet ./internal/config/` EXIT 0. (pkg/microservice fails only on CGO/librdkafka kafka symbols — host constraint, unrelated.)
- Known minor (display-layer, NOT a storage violation): approval/notification.go formats LINE-push timestamps with hardcoded ICT (UTC+7) instead of branch tz; defensible for a Thai-centric ERP, left as-is.
- Not deployed (backend redeploy + frontend .202 rebuild needed to observe; DEV deploy requires explicit "deploy dev").

- Tested the product-group tree screen CRUD end-to-end against live DEV (192.168.2.202): full lifecycle (create root/child, list-tree, get, rename Thai-UTF8, reparent, xsort reorder+swap, delete, verify-gone) all PASS.

  - #2 (HIGH): DeleteProductGroup / DeleteProductGroupByGUIDs only blocked on product-barcode ref, not child groups → deleting a parent silently orphaned children (FE re-roots dangling parentguid). FIX: block delete when a child exists (FindByDocIndentityGuid "parentguid"==guid; excludes soft-deleted), error "ไม่สามารถลบกลุ่มสินค้าที่มีกลุ่มย่อยได้ กรุณาย้ายหรือลบกลุ่มย่อยก่อน". (User chose block over cascade.)

## 2026-06-22 — Fix false-positive "ยังไม่ได้บันทึก" unsaved-changes guard (system-settings CRUD)
- Symptom (ลุงจืด): the unsaved-changes confirm dialog fired every time you switched records even WITHOUT editing anything.
- Root cause: frontend/src/app/system-settings/system-settings-screen.tsx had two guards keyed only on `formOpen` (openEdit ~line 1777, list onSelect ~line 3223): `if (formOpen && idChanged)` warned whenever a form was merely OPEN and another record was selected — no dirty check.
- Fix: added `isFormDirty` (useMemo) that compares the canonical payload of the current form vs its baseline — `buildPayload(form, editing, ...)` vs `buildPayload(baseline, ...)` where baseline = formFromRecord(editing) for edit mode or defaultForm(+parentguid) for create; raw-form fallback if workspace/auth null. Both guards now gated `formOpen && isFormDirty && idChanged`. buildPayload normalizes both sides (ignores readOnly fields like parentguid + transient shape), avoiding re-introducing false positives from benign mount normalization. Tree screens reuse the parent openEdit, so they are covered too.
- Verified: tsc --noEmit EXIT 0; production `next build` EXIT 0; live UI on Claude Preview (autoPort, fresh build → .202 backend): (1) open group A untouched → click edit B = NO dialog, switches directly (false-positive gone); (2) toggle the disabled checkbox on B (genuinely dirty) → click edit A = dialog DOES appear (real protection intact). Seed/cleanup of DEMO groups done; preview stopped.

## 2026-06-23 — Fix "กลุ่มสินค้า ย้ายไปมา หายหมด" (product-group tree vanishes after reparent)
- Symptom (ลุงจืด): dragging/reparenting product groups made ALL groups disappear from the tree.
- Root cause (verified via live API + UI): a reparent can create a CYCLE in parentguid (A→C→B→A). Confirmed empirically — backend has no cycle guard (#6, PUT accepts it, data NOT lost) and the FE move-guard checked `targetGroup.parentguidall` (a string that goes STALE on descendants after a move, so the cycle slips through). frontend/src/app/system-settings/product-group-tree-view.tsx treeRoots builder put every node with a present parent into childGroups and only parent-less nodes into `roots`; in a cycle EVERY node has a present parent → `roots = []` → tree renders empty = "หายหมด" (groups are fine in DB, just unrenderable).
- Fix (FE, minimal, 2 parts):
  1. Cycle-tolerant builder: walk parentguid upward with a visited-set (`chainReachesRoot`); a node whose chain loops never reaches a root → surface it as a root (flat) instead of dropping it, and never link cyclic nodes into childGroups (no render-recursion loop). Tree can no longer vanish regardless of cycle source.
  2. Cycle prevention: new `isWithinSubtreeOf(nodeGuid, ancestorGuid)` walks the LIVE override-aware parentguid graph (not the stale parentguidall string). moveGroupAsChild blocks when target is inside the dragged subtree; reorderGroup blocks when the new parent is the dragged node or inside its subtree.
- Verified: tsc EXIT 0; production next build EXIT 0; live Claude Preview (fresh build → .202) with a deliberately seeded cycle (A→C→B→A) → /productgroup renders all 3 cyclic groups flat as roots (5 rows incl. 2 pre-existing) with แก้ไข/ลบ — NOT empty. Pre-fix the same data rendered 0 rows. Cyclic test data cleaned up (break-cycle then delete; note the #2 child-guard blocks deleting cyclic nodes until parent is cleared).
- Recommend (optional, deferred earlier as #6): add a backend cycle guard in UpdateProductGroup so non-UI clients/API can't store a cycle. FE is now safe both ways (prevents + tolerates).

## 2026-06-30 (cont.) — Marketplace import: root producttype fix + raw-resave fix done; green-proof needs a real product
- TASK 1 (root producttype): FE `type ProductType = number` → struct `{guidfixed,code,names}` (`product-barcode/types.ts`); `rawToProductBarcode` now preserves the struct (`utils.ts`); empty default → `{code,names:[]}`; `product-barcode-screen.tsx` reads producttype.code (was getFirstNumber→0). (b) the list is a goapi read-projection that LAGS (shows already-deleted rows). DELETE works at the store when given the list guidfixed (verified: DELETE 200 → subsequent GET 400 not-found). The UI uses list guids so its deletes work; the stale-list display is the projection-lag symptom.
- GREEN-PROOF BLOCKER (not a screen bug): API-seeded barcodes have stored `names: []` because **barcode.names derives from the product master (itemguid)**; my seeds used a fake itemguid (no master) → empty names → the update-time `names must contain ≥1` validator fails on re-save. itemunitnames + producttype.names (sent inline) DID store. So the import save needs a REAL product (master+barcode, e.g. created via the Product UI), which I have not yet run. Code fixes are sound + verified compile; full green e2e still pending a real product.

## 2026-06-30 (cont.) — Clarified: barcode uniqueness WORKS; the "duplicates" were projection ghosts
- Jead flagged: barcode must be unique per holding — why did the tests show dups?
- VERIFIED uniqueness IS enforced: back-to-back create of the same barcode → create#1 201, create#2 **400 "barcode is exists"** (backend `productbarcode_http_service.go:138` rejects when FindByDocIndentityGuid finds an existing barcode in the holding). Projection showed count=1.
- So the earlier "duplicate" MEMEE/UITEST rows were **goapi list-projection GHOSTS**: my reseed scripts did delete-then-create; DELETE removed the row from the Mongo store (uniqueness check then passes → new doc created) but the **goapi read-projection (`/api/product/barcode/list`) does NOT drop deleted barcodes**, so it showed the deleted ghost + the new row = looked like a dup. Mongo store integrity is fine (no real dups).
- REAL underlying bug (single root for both "delete ไม่ลบ" and "barcode ซ้ำ" symptoms): **the goapi list read-projection is not updated on delete** (delete propagates to Mongo but the projection keeps stale rows). Creates DO reflect promptly. This is a CQRS projection/delete-propagation gap worth a backend look (Kafka projection pipeline) — NOT investigated yet.

## 2026-06-30 (cont.) — FIXED the "barcode ซ้ำ"/ghost bug (goapi list missing deletedat filter)
- Jead: screens show duplicate barcodes; find the projection-delete bug; rule = must check MongoDB every time.
- ROOT CAUSE (definitive): the frontend barcode list calls goapi `BarcodeListHandler` (`internal/goapi/handlers/barcode_list.go`), which reads MongoDB `productbarcodes` but its filter was `{holdingcode}` ONLY — **missing `deletedat: {$exists:false}`**. Soft delete sets `deletedat` (doc kept), so deleted barcodes stayed in the list as GHOSTS that looked like duplicates (uniqueness only blocks LIVE docs, verified: 2nd create of same barcode → 400 "barcode is exists"). mainapi `SearchRepository.FindStep` already filtered deletedat; this goapi handler didn't. Only this one goapi handler reads `productbarcodes` directly (audited).
- FIX: added `deletedat: {$exists:false}` to the filter (one filter var, used for find+count). Built+deployed to local mainapi via `deploy-mainapi-fast.ps1` (compile 12.7s, healthz OK).
- VERIFIED: clean ghost test — create→list(1)→delete→list now **0** (was 1 before fix), recreate same barcode 201. Ghost gone.
- RULE added: `.agents/rules/bc-account-core-rules.md` "Barcode / Soft-Delete Read Integrity" — every read of a soft-deleted collection via raw bson must filter `deletedat:{$exists:false}` (find AND count); read live MongoDB state.
- STILL PENDING: Task 2 (marketplace import green via real product) — API-seeded barcodes have empty `names` (derives from product master/itemguid which my seed faked); needs a product created via the real Product UI (or a seeded product master) to fully demo the producttype + raw-resave import fixes green.

My earlier seeds used a FAKE itemguid → master not found → names overwritten to [] → re-save failed `names must contain ≥1`. NOT a code bug — the marketplace import + producttype/raw-resave fixes are sound.
