## 2026-07-06 — เริ่มงาน ทดสอบ+ปรับปรุง+ออกแบบ สินค้า/บาร์โค้ด/สินค้าชุด (blast radius สูงมาก)
- Jead สั่ง "ทดสอบ ปรับปรุง ออกแบบ ฯลฯ" กลุ่มเมนู "สินค้าและบาร์โค้ด" 3 จอ: สินค้า, บาร์โค้ด, สินค้าชุด + ให้ดูของคู่แข่งด้วย
- **ตรวจ scope ก่อนเริ่ม (inline, สำคัญมาก)**: ทั้ง 3 จอเป็น custom screen ขนาดใหญ่ (`product-screen.tsx` ~3300 บรรทัด 12 tab เป็นจอที่ซับซ้อนที่สุดในระบบ, `product-barcode-screen.tsx` ~1618 บรรทัด, `product-set-screen.tsx` ~1994 บรรทัด) — **"สินค้าชุด" ไม่มี backend model แยกเลย** เป็นแค่ filter ฝั่ง UI บน Product model (`itemtype:2`/`materialtype:3`) คนละเรื่องกับโมดูล BOM ("สูตรผลิต") ที่มีอยู่แล้ว
- **Blast radius สูงมาก**: มีโมดูลอื่นอ้างอิง Product/ProductBarcode โดยตรงประมาณ 25 ตัว (saleinvoice, purchase, stock*, smlaiproduct/* 10 ตัว, POS, goapi handlers ฯลฯ) — การแก้ schema เสี่ยงพังหลายจุด ต่างจากงานก่อนหน้า (เจ้าหนี้/ลูกหนี้/cost-center) ที่ยังไม่มีใครใช้จริง
- **ยังไม่มี e2e test เลยสักตัว** สำหรับ 3 จอนี้ (ของเดิมมีแค่ BOM/category-list/warehouse)
- ตั้ง Workflow (task `wyjaolr1c`, run `wf_897ec762-8f7`) 4 phase: Research (UAT ทดสอบสภาพปัจจุบันจริง 3 จอ คู่ขนานกับ web competitor + ปรึกษา GPT/GLM/DeepSeek) → Design Review (Fable แยกชัดเจนเป็น **Category A** = ปลอดภัยทำได้เลย vs **Category B** = ต้องรอ Jead ตัดสินใจก่อน ไม่ implement เอง) → Implement (ทำเฉพาะ Category A) → Verify (UAT ครบ + เพิ่ม Playwright ให้ 3 จอที่ไม่เคยมี test)
- **จงใจออกแบบ workflow ให้ safe**: สั่งชัดว่าอะไรแตะ schema/blast radius สูงต้อง flag ไม่ใช่เดาทำเอง (เรียนจากบทเรียนงานก่อนหน้าเรื่อง Project/Job conflict และ branch routing decision)
- รอผลแล้วจะตรวจซ้ำเอง

## 2026-07-06 — UAT (Product screen) done: found + documented critical Product LIST bug, verified Create/Read/Update/Delete work, added Playwright regression
- **Environment correction (mine, first pass mistake, corrected):** local Docker `mongodb` container is NOT the real DEV database — `backend/bootstrap.json` `mongodb.uri` points to MongoDB Atlas (`dev.brulkiz.mongodb.net`, db `bcaiclouddb`). All verification redone against Atlas (reachable via `docker exec mongodb mongosh "mongodb+srv://...@dev.brulkiz.mongodb.net/bcaiclouddb?appName=dev"` — the local container has network egress even though its own local db is unused/empty).
- **CRITICAL FINDING (flagged, NOT fixed — wide blast radius + deliberate architecture choice, per task instructions and Dissent rule):** Product screen's LIST (every normal page load — `GET /api/product?...&holdingcode=X`) is proxied to a **PostgreSQL-backed** endpoint (`frontend/src/app/api/product/[[...productPath]]/route.ts` → `POST {goapi}/api/product/search` → `ProductSearchHandler` in `backend/internal/goapi/handlers/product_cache.go`), NOT the MongoDB `/product` endpoint. That query joins `public.product` + `public.productbarcode` + `public.inventorystockbalances` in a **per-tenant PostgreSQL database** (one DB per holdingcode). In this DEV Docker Postgres, `productbarcode`/`inventorystockbalances` tables do not exist for ANY tenant (only `product`, empty) → **every single list load 500s** (`{"error":"Query execution failed"}`), UI shows empty list + error toast. This was a deliberate switch (commit `75bca6e6 "Use product PG datalist and clean header context"`) and the project's own "No Silent Fallback" rule (`AGENTS.md`) forbids silently routing back to Mongo — so the visible error is correct behavior for a broken source, but the underlying PG projection pipeline being unpopulated/never-migrated is a real gap that needs a decision (populate the pipeline for DEV, or revert Product list to Mongo-backed). Also noticed the PG query doesn't even filter by `holdingcode` column despite the `productbarcode` migration (`add_productbarcode_materialtype_projection.sql`) defining `holdingcode` as part of its primary key — suggests `product_cache.go`'s query was written under a different (per-tenant-database) assumption than the migration's (shared-table) design; a second latent inconsistency worth Design Review attention.
- **Knock-on UX bug from the above:** `handleSave` in `product-screen.tsx` calls `loadProducts()` right after every successful Create/Update; when that reload 500s it clears `items` to `[]`, so the just-saved product visually vanishes from the screen (right pane resets to empty placeholder) even though the save itself succeeded. Same root cause, not separately fixed.
- **Verified GOOD (no bug):** Create/Read(by-guid)/Update/Delete all round-trip correctly UI+API+MongoDB(Atlas) — classification hierarchy (group/category/brand code+names) verified round-trip; soft-delete pattern (`deletedat`/`deletedby` set, doc retained, subsequent GET 404s) confirmed correct and intentional (matches `FindOneByCode`'s `deletedat: {$exists:false}` filter pattern). All ~11 detail tabs (basic/classification/units-barcode/BOM/stock/media/logistics/restaurant/time-for-sales/branch-business/misc) render and save without crashing.
- **Corrected my own false trail:** `Product.Barcodes[]`/`Prices[]` do NOT round-trip on PUT because `GetProduct` (`product_http_service.go`) intentionally IGNORES the stored `Barcodes` field on read and re-derives it live from the separate `productbarcodes` collection by item code — it's a computed read-model field, not something this screen's save path ever populates (`product-screen.tsx` has zero references to `prices`/`keynumber`/`barcodes` writes). Multi-tier pricing UI lives only on the Barcode screen, out of this test's real scope — task's own scope note about testing "multi-tier prices" on the Product screen doesn't apply; documented this precisely in the new spec so it isn't re-investigated as a false bug next time.
- **Product-group/category/brand picker modals are empty** in this tenant (`db.productgroups.countDocuments({}) == 0` etc.) — dev seed-data gap, not a code bug (`api/product-barcode/master/group` returns 200 with zero rows).
- Added `frontend/e2e/product-crud.spec.ts` (new, passing) — real UI login → Add form → touches all tabs → Save (verifies 201 via network wait) → GET/PUT/DELETE against `/api/product/:guid`, verifying classification round-trip and soft-delete 404 behavior; documents the list bug in file-level comments so it isn't silently "fixed" by reintroducing a Mongo fallback later without a real decision. Test data cleaned up (soft-deleted) after every run, zero live leftovers confirmed in Atlas.
- Did NOT touch product-barcode-screen.tsx, product-set-screen.tsx, or product-unit (all out of scope for this pass — Barcode/Product-Set UAT still pending as separate passes per the existing 4-phase workflow).

## 2026-07-06 (cont.) — UAT (Barcode screen) done: found + fixed a real hard-blocker bug (unit picker), verified full CRUD, added Playwright regression
- **Critical bug found + fixed (small, clearly-scoped, root-cause):** the required "หน่วยนับ" (unit) field on the barcode create/edit form could never be filled for ANY company — the unit picker (`MasterPicker` master="unit" → `/api/product-barcode/master/unit` → backend `GET /unit?companyguid=...`) always returned zero rows, even though the standalone หน่วยนับ master screen showed 109 real seeded units. Root cause: every unit doc has `companyguids: null` (meaning "available to all companies" per the field's intent), but `UnitHttpService.SearchUnit`/`SearchUnitLimit` (`backend/internal/product/unit/services/unit_http_service.go`) built the scope filter as `$or:[{companyguids:{$exists:false}},{companyguids:{$size:0}},{companyguids:companyGuid}]` — none of those 3 branches match a field whose value is explicitly Mongo `null` (`$exists:false` needs the key absent; `$size:0` needs an actual empty array; neither is true for `null`). Fixed by adding a 4th branch `{companyguids: nil}` to both filters (2-line diff, verified via `git diff --stat` = 1 file changed, 2 insertions). This was a full CREATE blocker: without a unit, the required field can never be satisfied and no barcode can ever be saved for this tenant.
- **Verification order (no shortcuts):** (1) reproduced via direct API call with/without `&companyguid=...` on the exact same query — 50 rows vs 0 rows; (2) read `unit_http_service.go` source to confirm the `$or` clause exactly; (3) confirmed via `/api/system-settings/productunit` that seeded units really do have `companyguids: null` (not missing/empty array); (4) applied the fix; (5) redeployed backend via `scripts/deploy-mainapi-fast.ps1` (34s fast binary-swap, `/healthz` OK); (6) re-ran the exact same API query — now 50 rows; (7) re-tested the real browser UI end-to-end — unit picker now shows units, barcode create succeeds; (8) confirmed an auto-created unit (via barcode-create's "unit doesn't exist yet, create it" path) also gets `companyguids: null` by default and is now correctly visible too (forward-compatible, not just a one-off patch for existing data).
- **Full CRUD verified UI + API + MongoDB(Atlas) agree, evidence for each:** CREATE (barcode E2EBC900001, unit=BOX, multi-tier prices 199/179 — verified via fresh `listBarcodes` API read after save, both tiers present in DB); READ (detail panel shows correct barcode/name/unit/price); UPDATE (changed tier-1 price 199→249 via UI, saved, re-read via API confirms 249 persisted, tier-2 untouched at 179); DELETE (confirm-dialog flow, then verified via fresh list = 0 rows AND direct `GET /product/barcode/:guid` = "document not found", not a soft-delete ghost).
- **Price history verified correct on both create and update:** create wrote 2 history rows (one per price tier, `action:"create"`, `oldprice:0`); update wrote exactly 1 new row for the changed tier only (`action:"update"`, `oldprice:199→newprice:249`) — no spurious rows for the untouched tier. History rows are retained after the barcode itself is deleted (intentional audit-trail behavior, not a bug).
- **Barcode uniqueness on CREATE verified correct:** duplicate barcode POST returns clean 400 `"barcode is exists"`, no corruption, and the frontend's `saveEditor` correctly surfaces the real backend message as an error toast (not swallowed) — confirmed by source read of the throw/catch path in `product-barcode-screen.tsx`.
- **Barcode picker modal (reused by Product Set) verified unaffected:** `product-set-screen.tsx`'s inline `BarcodePickerModal` calls the same `listBarcodes()` → `/api/product-barcode/list` used by the barcode screen's own list, which is scoped by `holdingcode` (not `companyguid`) — confirmed this path was never subject to the unit-filter bug and still returns correct results after the fix.
- **Documented, NOT fixed (flagged per Dissent rule — needs a product decision, not just a code patch):** the barcode field itself is fully editable in the edit-mode form (`barcode-form.tsx`, no `disabled`/`readOnly`, no `mode==="edit"` guard), but the backend's `UpdateProductBarcode` (`services/productbarcode_http_service.go`) unconditionally does `docData.Barcode = findDoc.Barcode` — silently discarding any barcode change a user makes while editing. Reproduced end-to-end: typed a new barcode value in the real UI, saved, got a "บันทึกบาร์โค้ดแล้ว" (saved successfully) toast with zero warning, but the DB still has the original barcode (confirmed via fresh API read). The UI does correctly re-sync to show the true value after save (no lingering stale display), so the user-visible harm is limited to one false "saved" confirmation for that field, not silent data corruption — but it's still a real trust/UX bug. Needs a decision: either disable+explain the barcode field in edit mode, or make barcode genuinely renameable (which would need a fresh uniqueness re-check on update, currently absent). Left unfixed because the right fix depends on a product call, not a code-only patch.
- **Small pre-existing debug leftovers noticed, NOT touched (out of this task's edit scope per Rule 4 — not created or made worse by this task):** stray `fmt.Printf("Updated docData: %+v\n", docData)` at `services/productbarcode_http_service.go:362` and a handful of other `fmt.Printf` debug lines scattered through the same file/`productbarcode_http.go` (pre-existing, not touched, not introduced by this change).
- Added `frontend/e2e/product-barcode-crud.spec.ts` (new, passing twice in a row for flakiness check) — real UI login → open บาร์โค้ด screen → create with unit-picker (regression-locks the companyguids:null fix) + 2-tier price → verify via direct MainAPI (not the Next.js `/api/product-barcode/list` proxy, which defaults to the remote 192.168.2.202 dev server unless `BCAI_LOCAL_BACKEND_URL` is set — not reliable for a deterministic local run) → duplicate-barcode rejection → price-history create+update entries → update via UI → delete via UI with confirm-dialog scoping (found + fixed a same-text-button collision in my own test helper: the detail panel's "ลบ" trigger and the confirm-dialog's own "ลบ" button share identical text, so the second click must be scoped to a descendant of the dialog container, not just re-matched globally) → verified gone via API. All test data cleaned up, zero leftovers confirmed via a final `E2EBC` prefix API sweep (also cleaned up one orphaned leftover barcode from an earlier failed test-development iteration, `E2EBC316338`).
- **Full e2e suite run:** 12 pre-existing failures across 5 unrelated spec files (`job-costing-channel-crud`, `master-brand-crud`, `product-category-list-crud`, `product-crud`, `product-warehouse-crud`) when run with parallel workers — re-ran each individually/solo and confirmed all but `master-brand-crud.spec.ts` pass in isolation (parallel-worker login-race flakiness, pre-existing test-infra issue, not caused by this change). `master-brand-crud.spec.ts` fails even solo, traced to a stale `/Test/` regex login pattern from an older commit (`8ccbcdbd`, predates this session) that no longer matches — a genuine pre-existing bug in that spec file, out of scope for this task, not fixed (flagged here instead per scope-drift rule).
- Backend build: `scripts/deploy-mainapi-fast.ps1` succeeded (34.1s fast binary-swap), `/healthz` OK both immediately after deploy and again at the end of this session. Frontend: `tsc --noEmit` clean (0 errors) including the new spec file.
- Did NOT touch product-screen.tsx or product-set-screen.tsx this pass (Product UAT already done in the prior worklog entry; Product Set UAT was done in an earlier session per `product-set-crud.spec.ts`'s existing file header). Did NOT redesign anything on the Barcode screen — this was a pure UAT + minimal-bug-fix pass per task instructions.

## 2026-07-06 (cont.) — UAT (Product Set screen) done: 2 real bugs found + fixed, safety checks all pass, Playwright regression added
- **Mechanism verified against real source first (per task instruction):** `product-set-screen.tsx` has no dedicated backend model — `loadProductSets` calls `GET /api/product?...&itemtype=2&materialtype=3` (no `holdingcode` query param, so it stays on the real MongoDB-backed `/product` route, NOT the PG-backed list path documented as broken in the Product-screen UAT above — confirmed this screen is unaffected by that issue). `handleSave` force-sets `itemtype: 2, materialtype: 3` on every payload regardless of what was loaded into the edit form. Backend `product_http_service.go` `Create`/`Update` both call `barcodeModel.ValidateProductClassification` (`productbarcode/models/product_classification.go`), which rejects itemtype=2 paired with any materialtype other than 3 — so classification is double-guarded (frontend force-set + backend validation), not just a client convention.
- **Safety questions from the task, all verified good:** (1) a regular product (itemtype 0) cannot appear in/be edited as a product set — proved both client-side (`.filter(item.itemtype === 2)`) and server-side (itemtype=2&materialtype=3 query filter actually excludes it, confirmed via raw API cross-check, not just trusting the UI filter); (2) deleting a set only removes that itemtype:2 record — confirmed an unrelated plain product survives untouched after a set delete, same `/product/:guid` DELETE endpoint used by both; (3) create actually persists itemtype:2/materialtype:3 in MongoDB — confirmed via fresh GET-by-guid, not just the 201 response; (4) update preserves itemtype:2/materialtype:3 — confirmed after a real rename+save round-trip.
- **Bug 1 (fixed): `CreateProduct`/`UpdateProduct` HTTP handlers returned 500 for ALL service-layer errors**, including `ValidateProductClassification` validation failures (client input error, should be 400) — same bug class already fixed for the warehouse handlers in a prior session (see 2026-07-03 entry). Reproduced live: PUT with itemtype=2+materialtype=0 returned `500` with the correct validation message but wrong status code. Fixed both handlers in `backend/internal/product/product/product_http.go` (`http.StatusInternalServerError` → `http.StatusBadRequest`), matching the existing precedent in `warehouse_http.go`'s Update handlers (which also blanket-return 400 for the whole service error, not split by error type — confirmed this is the real project convention here, not a partial fix). Verified: document was NEVER corrupted by the bad update even before this fix (itemtype/materialtype stayed 2/3) — this was a wrong-status-code bug, not a data-integrity bug. Rebuilt `mainapi` Docker image via `docker buildx build --load` (compose's plain `build` lacked BuildKit on this host — same workaround already documented in CLAUDE.md) and restarted the container; re-verified 400 with a fresh manual curl round-trip before re-running the full suite.
- **Bug 2 (fixed, more serious): `ProductSetScreen` destructured only `{ confirm }` from `useConfirmDialog()` and never rendered `{confirmationDialog}` anywhere in its JSX** — every other screen using this hook (`product-screen.tsx`, `product-barcode-screen.tsx`, `warehouse-tree-view.tsx`, `product-bom-editor.tsx`, `system-settings-screen.tsx`) correctly renders it; this one was the sole omission. Effect: clicking "ลบสินค้าชุด" (or the bulk-delete button) called `confirm({...})`, which returns a Promise that only resolves when the (never-mounted) dialog's own button calls `close()` — so the Promise **never resolves**, the delete silently does nothing, no error, no dialog, indistinguishable from a dead button. Found via a debug Playwright run with full DOM dump right after the click (both a raw JS `.click()` and Playwright's own trusted `getByRole().click()` were tried, ruling out a synthetic-event issue) — the confirm dialog never appeared in either case, and no rendered instance existed anywhere in the page. Root-caused to the missing render (not an event issue) by comparing against the 5 other working call sites. Fixed with a 2-line change: destructure `confirmationDialog` too, render `{confirmationDialog}` at the end of the component's JSX (same placement pattern as the other 5 screens). Rebuilt frontend (`tsc --noEmit` clean, then `next build && next start` per project convention, never `next dev`) and re-verified delete works end-to-end afterward.
- **Added `frontend/e2e/product-set-crud.spec.ts`** (new, passing) — real UI login (current `trade-partners-crud.spec.ts` retry-with-deadline convention, not the older fixed-`waitForTimeout` pattern that several pre-existing spec files still use) → Create via UI → verify itemtype/materialtype=2/3 via fresh API GET → verify a plain itemtype:0 product created directly via API never appears in the itemtype=2/materialtype=3 filtered list → attempt an invalid itemtype=2+materialtype=0 update via API and confirm it's rejected 400 without corrupting the doc → Update via UI (rename) and confirm itemtype/materialtype survive → Delete via UI and confirm only that record is removed, the unrelated plain product survives untouched. Cleans up all test data (set + plain product) in a `finally` block regardless of pass/fail.
- **Regression check:** ran the new spec standalone (pass), alongside `trade-partners-crud.spec.ts` with 2 workers (8/8 pass), and the full suite serially with `--workers=1` (this spec + `product-crud.spec.ts` + all of `trade-partners-crud.spec.ts` passed; only the same 5 pre-existing files already documented above as flaky-under-load — `job-costing-channel-crud`, `master-brand-crud`, `product-bom-crud`, `product-category-list-crud`, `product-warehouse-crud` — failed at their own login step, unrelated to this task's diff). Confirmed via this run that the flakiness is genuinely pre-existing and load-related, not something this session introduced.
- **Documented, not fixed (correctly out of scope per Dissent rule):** the ~25-module blast radius around the shared Product/ProductBarcode struct means any schema-level change to give Product Set its own identity/validation beyond the itemtype/materialtype convention would be R1 and needs Jead's explicit sign-off first; this pass only touched a status-code bug and a missing-render bug, neither of which changes the wire contract or schema.
- Cleanup: removed 2 throwaway debug spec files created while diagnosing the delete bug, confirmed zero leftover E2E/DEBUG/MANUALCHK test products remain in MongoDB Atlas, removed `frontend/test-results/` trace artifacts.

## 2026-07-07 — Jead แก้ความเข้าใจผิด: /product list ต้องใช้ PostgreSQL จริง (มียอดคงเหลือคำนวณเก็บไว้) → ไปแก้ sync pipeline แทน revert
- Jead ชี้แจง: หน้า Product แสดงยอดคงเหลือที่คำนวณเก็บไว้ (ไม่เหมือนจออื่น) จึงต้องอ่านจาก PostgreSQL จริงๆ ไม่ใช่ทางเลือกที่ผิด — ข้อมูลดิบยังอยู่ MongoDB ต้อง sync ผ่าน Kafka เข้า PostgreSQL ให้ถูกต้อง
- **ตรวจ scope ก่อนเริ่ม (inline)**: เจอ 2 root cause อิสระต่อกัน ทำให้ list พังใน DEV:
  1. **Kafka consumer ของ ProductBarcode ไม่เคย consume จริง** — โค้ด register ไว้ใน `main.go` ครบ (เหมือน consumer อื่นที่ทำงานได้จริง เช่น creditor/debtor/warehouse) แต่ `docker exec kafka kafka-consumer-groups --describe --group consumer-productbarcode-group-01` คืน `GroupIdNotFoundException` — ไม่เคย connect เลย ทั้งที่ Kafka topic `when-product-barcode-created` มี message จริงส่งเข้ามาตลอด (ยืนยัน consume message จริงได้)
  2. **DB provisioning ไม่สร้าง table ให้ tenant ใหม่อัตโนมัติ** — `createPostgreSQLDatabase()` รัน `CREATE DATABASE` เฉยๆ ไม่รัน DDL สร้าง table ต่อ (tenant ใหม่ `bctest01` มี 0 table); และ `inventorystockbalances` ไม่เคยอยู่ใน `DatabaseRebuildAll`'s table list เลย ถูกสร้างเฉพาะผ่าน manual endpoint `/inventory/create-tables` เท่านั้น (tenant เก่า `test` ที่มี 20 table แล้วก็ยังไม่มีตารางนี้)
- ตั้ง Workflow (task `wl4rognjh`, run `wf_f47c785c-a76`) 2 phase: Implement (debug หา root cause จริงของ consumer ที่ตายทำไม + แก้ auto-provisioning ให้สร้าง table ครบรวม inventorystockbalances) → Verify (สร้างสินค้าจริง ยืนยัน sync เข้า PostgreSQL จริง + หน้า /product โหลด list ได้จริงไม่ error 500)
- รอผลแล้วจะตรวจซ้ำเอง

## 2026-07-07 (cont.) — Independent verification of the Implement phase: both root causes confirmed genuinely fixed, 1 residual gap found (different code path, out of scope for this pass)
- **Verified root cause A (Kafka) independently, own commands, own data:** `docker exec kafka kafka-consumer-groups --describe --group consumer-productbarcode-group-01` → still `GroupIdNotFoundException` (confirms that group name is dead code, exactly as the implementer's report says — the real consumer is `biapi-inventory-consumer-v1`). `--describe --group biapi-inventory-consumer-v1` → all 3 barcode topics (`created`/`updated`/`deleted`) show LAG=0 with a real attached consumer-id/host. `git diff` on `setupconfig/loader.go` confirms the exact 4-line snake_case-key-normalization fix, nothing broader.
- **Verified root cause B independently:** Read `create-database.go` (gitignored but present) directly — confirms `DatabaseChecker` now always calls the idempotent `DatabaseRebuildAll` (process-lifetime `sync.Map` cache, not just "DB doesn't exist yet"), confirms `productbarcode`/`customer`/`debtor`/`creditor` all moved their pgvector `ALTER`/HNSW-index statements to a separate post-commit `db.ExecContext` (matches the "same-tx pgvector abort" bug description exactly), confirms `inventory.CreateInventoryCostingTables(targetDB)` is now called at the end of `DatabaseRebuildAll`. Live `psql -d test`: both `productbarcode` and `inventorystockbalances` tables are real and present (were missing before, per the original bug report). `holdingcode`→`holding_code` column rename in `handlers/kafka/inventory.go` confirmed via `git diff` (5 occurrences, matches report).
- **Full fresh UAT run by me (not reusing implementer's data):** ran `e2e/product-crud.spec.ts` myself against the live stack — it FAILED on its own pre-existing assertion `expect(bodyHasText(page,"Query execution failed")).toBe(true)` (the assertion that documented the bug as unfixed), which is itself proof the 500 is gone. Confirmed via fresh `docker logs mainapi` during my own test run: `POST /goapi/api/product/search ... status:200`. Updated the now-stale file-header comment + flipped that assertion to `.toBe(false)` (small, targeted edit, not a rewrite) and re-ran — now green. Also ran `product-barcode-crud.spec.ts` + `product-set-crud.spec.ts` (both pass) and `tsc --noEmit` (clean). Backend Docker-builder build (`CGO_ENABLED=1 go build -tags musl ./...`) exits 0.
- **1 residual gap found, correctly out of scope for this pass (flagged, not fixed):** `ProductSearchHandler` (`internal/goapi/handlers/product_cache.go`) calls `mypg.PgSqlFastConnect(holdingCode)` directly and never calls `build.DatabaseChecker`. `PgSqlFastConnect` → `PgSqlFastConnectV2` → `mydb.GetGlobalConnectionFromPool` → `mydb.DatabaseManager.connectPostgreSQL`, whose auto-create-on-missing-DB path (`createPostgreSQLDatabase` in `unified_database_manager.go:95`) still only runs a bare `CREATE DATABASE`, zero table DDL — this is a genuinely different code path from the Kafka-triggered `build.DatabaseRebuildAll` fixed in this task. Net effect: a tenant whose absolute first-ever backend interaction is loading `/product` (before any barcode/warehouse/purchase Kafka message has ever fired for that holdingcode) would still get an empty auto-created DB and the list would still 500 once. In practice this session every tested tenant (`test`) already had Kafka activity first, so it never reproduced live here — flagging per Dissent rule rather than fixing silently, since the right fix (should `ProductSearchHandler` call `DatabaseChecker` too, or should `mydb.createPostgreSQLDatabase` itself run full DDL) is a design decision, not obviously part of "root cause A/B" as scoped.
- **Confirmed no stray Go build-cache dirs** at repo root or `backend/` root.
- Did not commit or push anything (per task instruction).

## 2026-07-07 (cont.) — พบเพิ่มเอง: 10 ไฟล์ Go จริงไม่เคยถูก track ใน git มาตลอดเพราะ .gitignore ผิด — แก้แล้ว (Jead ยืนยัน)
- ระหว่างตรวจซ้ำ ผมเช็คว่าทำไม `create-database.go` (ไฟล์ที่เพิ่งแก้) ไม่โผล่ใน `git diff` เลย — พบว่า `.gitignore` มี pattern `**/build/` (ใต้ comment `# Flutter`) ที่ตั้งใจกันโฟลเดอร์ build-output ของ Flutter แต่ไปแมทช์โฟลเดอร์ source จริงชื่อ `backend/internal/goapi/process/build/` โดยไม่ตั้งใจ — เช็คแล้วไม่มีโปรเจกต์ Flutter จริงในเรพอนี้เลย (`pubspec.yaml` ไม่มี) กฎนี้จึงไม่มีประโยชน์อะไรแต่ทำร้ายจริง
- **ผลกระทบจริง**: 10 จาก 11 ไฟล์ในโฟลเดอร์นี้ (รวม `create-database.go` ที่เพิ่งแก้ bug สำคัญไปในงานนี้) ไม่เคยถูก commit เข้า git เลยตั้งแต่สร้างไฟล์ — โค้ดจริงที่ระบบใช้งานอยู่ไม่มีประวัติ ไม่มี backup ผ่าน git เลย
- **ถามลุงจืดแล้วยืนยันแก้เลย**: ลบบรรทัด `**/build/` ออกจาก `.gitignore` (เช็คแล้ว `.next/` มี rule แยกคุ้มครองอยู่แล้ว ไม่กระทบ Next.js build) + `git add` 10 ไฟล์เข้าระบบ **ไม่ commit เอง** (รอคำสั่งแยกถ้าต้องการ)
- **เช็คความปลอดภัยก่อน stage**: grep หา secret/password/token ในไฟล์ทั้ง 10 เจอ 1 จุดใน `build-erp-user.go` เป็นแค่ default seed password `"123456"` (มี comment ไทยกำกับว่า "ควรเปลี่ยนในการใช้งานจริง") ไม่ใช่ credential จริงที่รั่ว — ปลอดภัย stage ได้
- **สถานะปัจจุบัน**: `.gitignore` + 10 ไฟล์อยู่ใน git staging area (`git add` แล้ว) รอคำสั่ง commit จาก Jead ต่างหาก

## 2026-07-07 (cont.) — สร้างข้อมูลตัวอย่าง สินค้า/บาร์โค้ด/สินค้าชุด (ใช้ /uat-crud-mongo) + แก้ความเข้าใจผิดเรื่อง "กลุ่มสินค้า"
- Jead สั่ง `/uat-crud-mongo` "สร้างข้อมูลตัวอย่างให้ด้วย และให้สัมพันธ์กันด้วย ทำเหมือน user ป้อนเองเลย ตรวจด้วยทุก field ที่มี relation อ้างอิงครบหรือไม่ ถ้าไม่ครบ ไปสร้างจอเพิ่มได้เลย"
- **ตรวจ scope ก่อนเริ่ม**: relation field บน Product/ProductBarcode มี 10 ตัว (unit/group/groupsubone/groupsubtwo/brand/design/model/pattern/grade/category) — พบว่า master collection เกือบทั้งหมด (ยกเว้น unit ที่มี 110 doc) **ว่างเปล่าไม่มี document เลยสักตัว** (ไม่ใช่แค่ว่างสำหรับ tenant นี้ — collection ยังไม่เคยถูกสร้างเลย) ทำให้ product จริงที่ user จะสร้างไม่มีตัวเลือกให้กดในหลาย field — และ groupsubone/groupsubtwo **ไม่มีจอ frontend เลย** (มีแค่ backend + proxy wiring)
- ตั้ง Workflow แรก (task `wm6fojkru`) แต่ Jead แทรกก่อนที่จะไปไกล ชี้ว่า **"กลุ่มสินค้า" (Product Group) เป็นโครงสร้างแบบ tree** — เช็คจริงพบว่าถูกต้อง: `productgroup` model มี `ParentGUID`/`ParentGUIDAll`/`ChildCount`/`XSorts` จริง และจอ `/productgroup` ที่มีอยู่แล้วก็เป็น tree UI จริง (subtitle เขียนไว้ชัดว่า "จัดกลุ่มสินค้าแบบลำดับชั้น...ลากวางจัดลำดับได้") — ผมพลาดไม่ทันสังเกตจุดนี้ตอน scope รอบแรก (workflow เดิมไม่ทันมีการเปลี่ยนแปลงไฟล์จริงใดๆ ก่อนถูก interrupt จึงไม่มีอะไรต้อง revert)
- **ขุดเจอเพิ่มระหว่างตรวจสอบ**: GroupSubOne/GroupSubTwo จริงๆ เป็นคนละ hierarchy กับ "กลุ่มสินค้า" — เป็น 3 ชั้นแยกต่างหากในตระกูล `smlaiproduct` (Group Main → GroupSubOne → GroupSubTwo ผ่าน `GroupMainGuid`/`GroupSubGuid`) แต่ field เหล่านี้ optional (ไม่มี `validate:"required"`) และ Product เองไม่เคยอ้างอิง Group Main โดยตรงเลย (เช็คจาก `tab-product-classification.tsx` จริง) — ตัดสินใจ **ไม่สร้างจอที่ 3 สำหรับ Group Main** ในรอบนี้ (เกินความจำเป็นจริง) แค่สร้าง GroupSubOne/GroupSubTwo แบบ flat Code+Names เหมือน sibling อื่น เว้น field parent-link ไว้ก่อน
- แก้ workflow script ใหม่ให้สั่งสร้าง "group" เป็น tree จริง (root group 2 กลุ่ม + child 2-3 กลุ่มต่อ root) แทนการสร้างแบบ flat แล้ว relaunch (task `wh13777rg`, run `wf_6d7cdf81-cbf`)
- **Jead แทรกซ้ำอีกรอบ** ก่อน workflow ไปไกล ชี้สกรีนช็อตกลุ่มเมนู "จัดกลุ่มสินค้า" (กลุ่มสินค้า/จัดหมวดสินค้า/สินค้าในหมวด) บอกว่า "หน้าจอมีแล้ว ตรวจแล้วด้วย เอาตัวนี้ไปใช้เลย" — หยุด workflow (`wh13777rg`) ทันที (ยังไม่ทันแตะไฟล์ใดๆ) แล้วตรวจจริง พบว่า **"จัดหมวดสินค้า"/"สินค้าในหมวด" (`/productcategorygroupselectscreen`+`/productcategorylist`, basePath `/product/category`) เป็นระบบ category คนละตัวกับที่วางแผนไว้** — `productcategories` collection ก็เป็น tree เหมือน `productgroups` (ParentGUID/ChildCount/XSorts) **แต่มี `CodeList` เก็บรายการ product code ที่เป็นสมาชิกของหมวดนั้นด้วย** (ความสัมพันธ์กลับด้าน: หมวด→รายการสินค้า ไม่ใช่ product มี field ชี้ไปหมวด) — แยกจาก `categoryproductmaster`/`/aicloud/category` (หมวดคุณลักษณะสินค้า, flat, คือ field `CategoryGuid` บน Product) ที่วางแผนไว้เดิม ทั้งสองระบบเป็นของจริงใช้งานได้ทั้งคู่ ไม่ใช่ตัวใดตัวหนึ่งผิด
- แก้ workflow อีกรอบ: เพิ่มการ seed `productcategories` เป็น tree จริง (ในขั้น seed master data) + เพิ่มขั้นตอนเอา product code จริงที่สร้างแล้วไปใส่ใน `codelist` ของหมวดจริงผ่านหน้า "สินค้าในหมวด" (ในขั้น seed products เพราะต้องรอมี product จริงก่อน) แล้ว relaunch (task `wbxe7pjqu`, run `wf_23a6d1b1-8b9`)
- รอผลแล้วจะตรวจซ้ำเอง

## 2026-07-07 (cont.) — Independent verification of sample-data task: master data + relations all confirmed real, but found+reproduced a genuine live blocker on `/product` list screen (not fixed, flagged per Dissent rule)
- **Isolated diff re-check (groupsubone/groupsubtwo screens):** confirmed via `git diff` the 2 new `aicloudConfig(...)` entries in `frontend/src/lib/system-setting-screens.ts` + 2 menu entries in `frontend/src/lib/menu-data.ts` are exactly the additive, sibling-pattern-matching change reported — no unrelated changes mixed in on my read. Confirmed the large unrelated creditor/debtor/bank-account/thai-address diff sitting in the same working tree (system-setting-screens.ts, menu-data.ts, product-barcode master route.ts) is a separate task's uncommitted work, untouched by me.

Caught the same "groupnumber:0 orphan default root" trap the original report flagged (my first raw Mongo query without a `groupnumber` filter matched the wrong empty "กาแฟ" doc) — corrected using `guidfixed` cross-reference, same lesson as the earlier session.
- **Independently re-verified 2 products + both Product Sets end-to-end (UI + fresh API + real MongoDB + real PostgreSQL):** `SMPCOFFEE1152349`/`SMPMILK1152349` — fresh `GET /product/:guid` AND raw MongoDB doc both show all 10 classification fields resolved to real Phase-2 master codes (cross-referenced each code against the actual master list, not just non-empty). `productbarcodes` Mongo doc for `SMPBCCOFFEE1152349` confirmed real `itemguid`/`itemcode` link. **PostgreSQL** (`docker exec postgres psql -d test`): `productbarcode` table has all 6 sample barcodes with correct `holding_code='test'` (Kafka sync pipeline fixed in a prior task this session is working correctly for barcode-create). `product` PG table confirmed to have 0 rows tenant-wide (not specific to sample data — this table is only ever populated by inventory/stock-movement Kafka events, never by product-master create, so this is expected/consistent, not a regression). Product Sets `SMPSETCOFFEE152349`/`SMPSETPARTY152349`: real MongoDB doc confirms `itemtype:2/materialtype:3` + `options[0].choices[].refbarcode` contains exactly the reported component barcodes.
- **Live browser re-verification, real fresh login (holding→company→branch, not reused session):** `/productbarcode` (บาร์โค้ด) screen genuinely lists all 8 real barcodes incl. all 6 sample ones; clicked `SMPBCCOFFEE1152349` — detail panel visibly renders every one of the 10 resolved classification labels (กลุ่มสินค้า, ยี่ห้อ, หมวดสินค้า, Class, Design, Grade, Model, Pattern, กลุ่มย่อย 1/2), exactly as a real user would see it. `/productset` (สินค้าชุด) screen lists all 3 sets incl. both sample ones with correct Thai names.
- **REAL BUG FOUND + REPRODUCED LIVE (flagged, NOT fixed — matches a residual gap already dissented/deferred in this session's own prior worklog entry, so treated as a design decision requiring Jead's input, not a silent fix):** the real **`/product` (สินค้า) list screen shows 0/0 "ไม่พบข้อมูลสินค้า" for the ENTIRE tenant**, including all pre-existing products, not just the new sample batch — reproduced with a genuinely fresh browser login (cleared localStorage, re-did the full เข้าทดสอบระบบ→เลือกกลุ่มกิจการ→เลือกบริษัท→เลือกสาขา flow), so this is not a stale-token artifact. Root cause traced by reading source directly: `frontend/src/app/api/product/[[...productPath]]/route.ts` GET handler — whenever the query string carries a `holdingcode` (which the real `/product` screen's `loadProducts()` in `product-screen.tsx` always sends), it unconditionally routes to `proxyProductPgListJson` → `POST /goapi/api/product/search` (`ProductSearchHandler` in `backend/internal/goapi/handlers/product_cache.go`), a **PostgreSQL-only** search whose query is `FROM public.product p LEFT JOIN productbarcode pb ...` — i.e. driven FROM the `product` table, not `productbarcode`. Confirmed via direct `psql`: PG `product` table = 0 rows tenant-wide (only ever populated by inventory/stock-movement Kafka events, never by product-master create — same table already checked empty during the Products/Barcodes verification above). Net effect: even though the real MongoDB `products`/`productbarcodes` collections and the PG `productbarcode` projection are all correctly populated, **no product that has never had a stock transaction can ever appear in the real `/product` screen**, for ANY tenant, not just this session's sample data. This is the exact same code path (`ProductSearchHandler`/`mypg.PgSqlFastConnect` bypassing `build.DatabaseChecker`) already flagged as a residual, deliberately-out-of-scope gap in this session's earlier Kafka/DB-provisioning fix entry — re-confirmed here as a live, reproducible, tenant-wide blocker, not a one-off. Did not fix — this needs a design decision (should `/product`'s list view read from Mongo like `/productbarcode` does, or should the PG projection also populate on product-master create, or should `ProductSearchHandler` fall back to Mongo when PG has 0 rows) rather than a patch applied silently mid-verification-task.
- **Playwright regression suite:** `product-crud.spec.ts` + `product-barcode-crud.spec.ts` + `product-set-crud.spec.ts` run together in isolation (3 workers) — all 3 PASS. Full suite run (`--workers=3` then retried `--workers=1`) shows 11-13 failures across `job-costing-channel-crud`, `master-brand-crud`, `product-bom-crud`, `product-category-list-crud`, `product-warehouse-crud`, and even `product-crud.spec.ts` itself on the serial rerun — all failing on the exact same generic login-helper assertion (`No clickable element found matching /Test/`), pointing to pre-existing test-environment flakiness (holding-list growth/reordering across repeated runs, not a regression caused by this session's screens/data) rather than a real product bug; not chased further as out of this task's scope. Re-running `product-barcode-sample-data.spec.ts` (the one-shot seeder) against already-seeded `152349` data timed out at its first step (expected/correct — it is a one-shot seeder, not designed for repeat runs against pre-existing codes with the same theme, and its job was already done in the prior session).
- **Final sanity re-checks:** `node node_modules/typescript/bin/tsc --noEmit` — clean, exit 0. Backend Docker-builder build (`CGO_ENABLED=1 go build -tags musl ./...`) — exit 0.
- **Confirmed:** the two-inconsistent-copies-of-dev-data-seeder-skill finding from the prior report (`.agents/skills/dev-data-seeder/` vs `.claude/skills/dev-data-seeder/`, differing field-naming convention) still stands — flagged again here as a docs/skill-maintenance item for Jead to reconcile later, not fixed in this pass (out of scope).

## 2026-07-07 (cont.) — เจ้าของสั่งปรึกษาแล้วแก้เลย: root cause ของบั๊ก /product ว่างทั้ง tenant
- Jead สั่ง "ปรึกษากันดู แล้วแก้เลย" สำหรับบั๊ก `/product` list ที่พบระหว่างงาน seed ข้อมูลตัวอย่าง
- **ตรวจ scope ก่อนปรึกษา (inline)**: ยืนยันชัดเจนว่า Product module (`backend/internal/product/product/`) **ไม่มี Kafka event pipeline เลยสักตัว** (ไม่มี producer เลย ต่างจาก ProductBarcode ที่มี pipeline ทำงานสมบูรณ์) — ตาราง PostgreSQL `product` ถูกสร้างไว้ถูกต้องแล้ว (schema ตรง) แต่ไม่เคยมีอะไรเขียนเข้าไปเลย เพราะไม่มี consumer ที่ทำหน้าที่นี้ (`handlers/kafka/` ไม่มีไฟล์ "product.go" คู่กับ "inventory.go" ที่จริงๆ คือ consumer ของ ProductBarcode) — query ที่ใช้แสดง list เป็น `FROM product LEFT JOIN productbarcode` ทำให้ `product` ว่าง = list ว่างทั้งหมด แม้ `productbarcode` จะมีข้อมูลจริงอยู่ 7 แถวก็ตาม
- ตั้ง Workflow ปรึกษา+แก้ (task `w62p9h7g8`, run `wf_b11468d1-69b`) 3 phase: Consult (DeepSeek/GLM/GPT คู่ขนาน + Fable สังเคราะห์ตัดสินใจ design ท่อ Kafka ใหม่สำหรับ Product) → Implement (สร้าง producer+consumer เลียนแบบ pattern ProductBarcode ที่ทำงานอยู่แล้ว) → Verify (สร้าง/แก้/ลบ product จริง ยืนยัน sync เข้า PostgreSQL + หน้า /product ใช้งานได้จริง)
- **บทเรียนเขียน workflow script**: เจอ syntax พลาดจริง (stray apostrophe ปิด string ก่อนเวลา) ที่ `node --check` ไม่จับได้แต่ทดสอบแยกส่วนแล้วพังจริง — แก้ก่อน launch โดยไม่พึ่ง `node --check` อย่างเดียวอีกต่อไป ต้องอ่านเนื้อหาจริงประกอบด้วย
- รอผลแล้วจะตรวจซ้ำเอง
- Did not commit or push anything. Did not delete any of the Phase 2/3 sample data (kept as the actual deliverable).

- **Diff re-check**: `git diff` for `backend/internal/product/product/`, `backend/internal/goapi/handlers/kafka.go`, `kafka_bridge.go`, plus new files `backend/internal/goapi/handlers/kafka/product.go`, `backend/internal/product/product/config/product_messagequeue_config.go`, `repositories/product_messagequeue_repository.go` all match the implementer's report exactly — additive Kafka producer/consumer pipeline mirroring the working ProductBarcode pattern, plus a `POST /product/resync` endpoint. `IProductRepository.FindFilter` addition confirmed safe: only one implementer in the whole backend (productbarcode module has its own unrelated `IProductRepository` in a different package).
- **Fresh product create (own test data, `VERIFY-TEST-01`, not reused from implementer)** via real UI (dev test-login → tenant `test` → บริษัท อรุณพาณิชย์ ทดสอบ จำกัด → สำนักงานใหญ่, same as prior session) → `POST /api/product → 201`. Own `docker exec postgres psql -d test` query confirmed the row landed in `product` (`name0` correct, `balanceqty=0`).
- **Update via UI** (real "แก้ไข" button → changed name → "บันทึก") → `PUT /api/product/VERIFY-TEST-01 → 200`. Own psql query confirmed `name0` updated in PG, `balanceqty` untouched (0).
- **Delete via UI** (real "ลบ" button → real confirm dialog "ต้องการลบบาร์โค้ดที่เลือกจริงหรือไม่") → `DELETE /api/product/VERIFY-TEST-01 → 200`. Own psql query confirmed hard delete (0 rows for that itemcode, PG `product` count back to 28). Mainapi container log independently confirmed the exact consumer log line the report quoted: `Deleted 1 product row(s) for itemcode VERIFY-TEST-01 (PG)`.
- **`/product` screen loaded fresh in browser**: genuinely renders real rows — "28 / 28 รายการ" (matches the resync count), and all 8 prior-session "152349" sample products visible (16 text occurrences, matching the report exactly).
- **REAL BUG FOUND + REPRODUCED LIVE (pre-existing, NOT part of this task's diff, not fixed — flagged per Dissent rule):** immediately after the delete above, searching `/product` for `VERIFY-TEST-01` (and even clicking "รีเฟรช") kept returning the already-deleted row for several minutes, with the API response literally showing `"source":"pgsql"` for a row proven to be gone from PostgreSQL. Root-caused by reading `backend/internal/goapi/handlers/product_cache.go`: `ProductSearchHandler` uses a package-level in-memory `productCache` (`NewProductCache(1000, 5*time.Minute)`) keyed by query, and the only code that ever calls `productCache.Clear()`/`ClearByPrefix()` is a separate manual admin endpoint (`ProductCacheClearHandler`) — nothing in the product create/update/delete path (neither the old mainapi service nor this session's new Kafka consumer) invalidates it. Confirmed conclusively by waiting out the full 5-minute TTL and reloading: the stale row disappeared and the list correctly showed "0 / 0 รายการ" for that query, then "28 / 28 รายการ" with the search cleared. `git diff --stat -- backend/internal/goapi/handlers/product_cache.go` shows zero changes — this bug predates and is untouched by this session's fix; it just happens to be the next thing a user would hit right after using the newly-fixed create/update/delete flow, so flagging it here rather than opening a separate silent fix.
- **Playwright regression**: `product-crud.spec.ts`, `product-barcode-crud.spec.ts`, `product-set-crud.spec.ts` run together (3 workers) — all 3 **PASS** (`node node_modules/playwright/cli.js test ... --workers=3` — `npx playwright test` failed to resolve on this shell, used the cli.js entrypoint directly as a workaround). `product-barcode-sample-data.spec.ts` confirmed still present but not re-run (one-shot seeder, already executed in the prior session per its own file-level comment).
- **Fixed a real stale-assertion bug in `product-crud.spec.ts`** per this task's explicit instruction: 3 in-body comments (lines ~148, ~198, ~211) still described the list as "expected to be empty/broken" / "known bug ... post-save list refresh 500s" even though the file-level comment already said "FIXED 2026-07-07". Updated all 3 to reflect the current, correct state and to accurately reference the newly-found stale-cache issue instead of the old (now-fixed) 500 error, so the two bugs aren't conflated. Re-ran `product-crud.spec.ts` alone after the edit — still **PASS**.
- **Final backend build**: `CGO_ENABLED=1 go build -tags musl ./...` via the Docker builder image → exit 0, no errors. No stray Go build cache directories found in the repo root (`__pycache__/` present is pre-existing/unrelated, not Go, not touched by this task).

## 2026-07-07 (cont.) — ปิดจ๊อบต่อเอง: แก้บั๊ก cache ค้าง 5 นาทีที่ verify phase เจอ (ไม่ใช่ปล่อยไว้)
- Verify phase เจอบั๊กใหม่: `productCache` (in-memory, TTL 5 นาที) **ไม่เคยถูกล้างตอนมีการสร้าง/แก้/ลบสินค้าหรือบาร์โค้ดเลย** — ทดสอบจริง: ลบสินค้าไปแล้ว API list ยังคืนข้อมูลเดิมนานเต็ม 5 นาทีถึงจะหายเอง — เป็นบั๊กที่ทำให้ fix หลักที่เพิ่งทำ (Kafka sync) ดูเหมือน "ไม่ทำงาน" ในสายตาผู้ใช้จริง เพราะเห็นผลช้าเกินไป
- **ตรวจเองแล้วตัดสินใจแก้ต่อทันที** (ไม่ทิ้งไว้เป็น decision point เพราะเป็น bug เล็ก เข้าใจ root cause ชัดแล้ว): พบว่า `productCache` เป็น package-private (`handlers` package) และ consumer ใหม่ (`handlers/kafka/product.go`) อยู่คนละ package เรียกตรงไม่ได้ (`handlers` import `handlers/kafka` อยู่แล้ว ถ้ากลับทางจะเกิด import cycle) — แก้โดยเรียก `productCache.Clear()` จาก `kafka_bridge.go` (ซึ่งอยู่ package `handlers` อยู่แล้ว) หลัง call consumer function สำเร็จ ทั้ง 6 จุด (Product create/update, Product delete, Barcode create/update, Barcode delete, Barcode bulk 2 จุด) — ครอบคลุมทุก write path ที่กระทบ query ร่วม (`product`+`productbarcode`+`inventorystockbalances`)
- **หมายเหตุสำคัญที่เจอระหว่างวิเคราะห์**: `ClearByPrefix` ที่มีอยู่เดิม**ใช้ไม่ได้จริงตามที่ดูเหมือน** เพราะ cache key เป็น SHA256 hash ของ `holdingcode_search_branch_...` ไม่ใช่ plain string prefix — เลยเลือกใช้ `Clear()` (ล้างทั้งหมดทุก tenant) แทน ปลอดภัยและง่ายกว่าตามหลัก minimal-fix (ระบบยังไม่มีลูกค้าจริง เขียนโหลดต่ำ)
- **Build + deploy + verify จริงเอง**: build ผ่าน Docker builder (exit 0) → rebuild image ผ่าน buildx workaround → restart container จริง → ทดสอบสด: query list (นับได้ 28) → สร้างสินค้าใหม่ผ่าน API ตรง → query list ทันทีอีกครั้ง (ไม่รอ) → **เห็น 29 ทันที ไม่ต้องรอ 5 นาที** ยืนยัน cache invalidation ทำงานจริง → ลบ test data ทิ้ง กลับเป็น 28 ตามเดิม
- **Regression**: รัน e2e 3 ไฟล์ (`product-crud`/`product-barcode-crud`/`product-set-crud`) — 2/3 ผ่าน, 1 ไฟล์ (`product-crud.spec.ts`) fail ที่ **ขั้นตอน login** (ชื่อกลุ่มกิจการจริงในระบบคือ "Test"/"test" แต่ helper รอ regex `/ทดสอบ/`) — ตรวจแล้วเป็นบั๊ก pre-existing เดิมที่ track แยกไว้แล้วตั้งแต่ก่อนหน้า ไม่เกี่ยวกับ cache fix รอบนี้ (โค้ดที่แก้ไม่แตะ login/holding-selection เลย)
- ยังไม่ commit/push ตามที่ไม่ได้สั่ง

## 2026-07-08 — บั๊กรูปภาพสินค้า/บาร์โค้ดไม่แสดง (แจ้งด้วย /uat-crud-mongo)
- Jead แจ้งรูปภาพหลักของสินค้าไม่ขึ้น (broken icon) พร้อม path `/goapi/s3/file/test/products/...` — ทดสอบซ้ำ 2 รอบด้วยรูปที่อัปโหลดใหม่ ยังพังเหมือนเดิมทั้งคู่ (reproducible จริง ไม่ใช่แค่ครั้งเดียว)
- **ตรวจ scope ก่อนแก้ (inline, 2 รอบ)**: เจอ root cause จริง 2 ชั้น —
  1. **บั๊กระบบกว้าง (หลัก)**: route `/goapi/s3/file/*` ต้องมี Authorization header (ยืนยัน 401 ถ้าไม่มี token) แต่ frontend render รูปด้วย `<img src="...">` ธรรมดา ซึ่ง **browser แนบ custom header ให้ `<img>` ไม่ได้เลย** — ทำให้รูปที่อัปโหลดสำเร็จจริงก็ยังพังเสมอ กระทบ 5 จุดในโค้ด (`tab-product-media.tsx` รูปหลัก+แกลเลอรี, `product-screen.tsx` thumbnail, `barcode-form.tsx` 4 จุด, `product-barcode-screen.tsx` thumbnail) — ทั้งที่มี pattern ที่ถูกต้องอยู่แล้ว (`useLogoImage()` hook + `imageNeedsAuthenticatedFetch()` ใน `logo-avatar.tsx`/`image-upload-proxy.ts`) ใช้กับโลโก้บริษัทเท่านั้น ไม่เคยเอามาใช้กับรูปสินค้า/บาร์โค้ด
  2. **บั๊กเจาะจงไฟล์เดิม**: ไฟล์ `test/products/20260707_235545_342e6dbb` ที่ Jead แจ้งตอนแรก ทดสอบด้วย token จริงแล้วยัง 404 "File not found" จริง — ไม่มี record ไหนใน MongoDB มี imageuri ผูกอยู่เลย (น่าจะเป็นค่าที่ค้างจาก form ที่ยังไม่ save หรืออัปโหลดที่ไม่สำเร็จจริง) — กำลังตรวจแยกว่า upload endpoint มีบั๊ก silent-failure จริงหรือไม่
- ตั้ง Workflow แก้ (task `wwahk8apn`, run `wf_296557f6-94d`) 2 phase: Implement (เอา pattern `useLogoImage()` ที่พิสูจน์แล้วว่าใช้งานได้จริงมาใช้กับทั้ง 5 จุดที่พัง) → Verify (อัปโหลดรูปจริงผ่าน UI ทดสอบว่าขึ้นทันทีไม่ต้อง reload + เพิ่ม Playwright regression กันบั๊กกลับมา)
- **บทเรียนซ้ำ**: เจอ syntax พลาดแบบเดียวกับก่อนหน้า (double apostrophe `''` ปิด string ก่อนเวลา) ที่ `node --check` ไม่จับอีกครั้ง — ต้อง grep หา pattern `[a-zA-Z]''|''[a-zA-Z]` เพิ่มเติมทุกครั้งก่อน launch จากนี้ไป ไม่พึ่ง `node --check` อย่างเดียว

## 2026-07-08 (cont.) — Independent verification of the image-auth-fix (workflow `wwahk8apn`): Implement phase confirmed genuinely done + closed the missing Verify-phase regression-test gap myself
- **`git status`/`git diff` re-check**: all 5 confirmed-broken locations now use a new shared `AuthenticatedImg` component (`frontend/src/components/authenticated-image.tsx`, new file) instead of a plain `<img src>` — `tab-product-media.tsx` (2 usages: main+gallery), `product-screen.tsx` (1, list thumbnail), `product-barcode-screen.tsx` (1, list thumbnail), `barcode-form.tsx` (4, main+gallery ×2 spots) = 8 call sites total, matching the original scope exactly.
- **Component design check (read the file directly, not just diff stat)**: `AuthenticatedImg`/`useAuthenticatedImageDisplaySource` is a clean generalization of the existing `useLogoImage()` pattern — fetches protected `/s3/file/` paths with the bearer token via `imageNeedsAuthenticatedFetch()` (already-existing helper, unchanged), renders a cached (LRU, 80-entry) `URL.createObjectURL()` blob, passes public URLs straight through with no fetch. Correctly placed in a `"use client"` component file (not the shared lib, which is also imported by server-only Route Handlers) — sound reasoning, not just copied blindly.
- **Live browser re-test (real UI, not just reading code)**: opened the actual running frontend, found the in-session browser had already run a real upload+display round trip during the workflow's own Verify pass — `POST /api/product-barcode/image → 200` → `GET .../backend/goapi/s3/file/test/products/...png → 200` → `blob:... → 200` — confirmed via `preview_network`, i.e. an uploaded image displays immediately with no page reload, which is the exact bug that was reported ("รูปยังไม่แสดง"). Also confirmed the empty-image case (`IMGTEST679410`, no `imageuri`) correctly renders the "ไม่มีรูปภาพ" fallback text, not a broken-icon `<img>` — both the happy path and the empty path are handled.
- **Minor non-blocking finding (not fixed, documented only)**: on first paint before `auth.backendUrl` populates, `AuthenticatedImg` can fire one authenticated `fetch()` at the un-prefixed path (`/goapi/s3/file/...` instead of `/backend/goapi/s3/file/...`), which 404s, immediately followed by a second correct request that succeeds once `auth` is ready (`useEffect` dependency array already re-fires on `authBackendUrl` change). Net effect: zero user-visible impact (no permanent broken state, self-heals within the same render cycle) — left as-is per minimal-change discipline, not worth a special-case guard for a cosmetic double-fetch pre-launch.
- **Gap found + closed myself**: the plan committed to "Verify phase: อัปโหลดรูปจริงผ่าน UI ... + เพิ่ม Playwright regression กันบั๊กกลับมา", but no new/updated regression assertion for image display existed anywhere in `frontend/e2e/*.spec.ts` (`grep -rl "AuthenticatedImg|s3/file|imageuri"` returned nothing). Per this project's standing convention (every verified bug fix gets a regression test) and Rule 2 (verify before done), added one myself to the existing `product-crud.spec.ts` create flow rather than a new file (reuses the existing login/product-create scaffolding): opens "ภาพและสี" tab, `setInputFiles` a real image (`public/flags/th.png`, already-existing 576-byte fixture, no new binary asset added), asserts an `img[src^='blob:']` becomes visible with `naturalWidth > 0` — directly reproduces and guards the exact bug class (plain `<img>` can never carry the auth header a protected path needs).
- **Also fixed, while making the above test runnable**: `product-crud.spec.ts`'s login helper waited for holding-group text `/ทดสอบ/` that never matches this environment's real holding name/code ("Test"/"test") — this exact pre-existing gap was already noted-but-left-open in the 2026-07-07 entry above. Fixed the regex + button-match to `/Test/i` / `startsWith("Test")` so the spec can actually run end-to-end instead of always timing out at login.
- **Full run**: `product-crud.spec.ts` + `product-barcode-crud.spec.ts` + `product-set-crud.spec.ts` together (3 workers) — **all 3 PASS**, including the new image assertion.
- **Also handled**: Jead reported `localhost:3000` down twice during this session; both times were the sandbox's known recurring dev-server-dies-between-tool-calls issue (not a code bug) — resolved via `preview_start`/rebuild each time, confirmed back up via `curl`+browser screenshot with zero console errors before continuing.
- Advisor scorecard: no advisor consulted this pass (pure independent re-verification + closing a known test gap, no design decision needed).

## 2026-07-08 (cont.) — Second independent verification pass of the image-auth-fix (fresh own data, own MongoDB/curl checks): confirmed real, PLUS a genuine new barcode-save bug found + regression test added
- **`git diff` re-check**: confirmed the diff is exactly the 8 `<img>`→`AuthenticatedImg` call sites + the new `frontend/src/components/authenticated-image.tsx` file, matching the prior verification pass above. `frontend/src/lib/image-upload-proxy.ts` diff is genuinely 0 lines (touched-then-reverted, as claimed).
- **Fresh product test, own data (`VERIFY907`, not reusing `IMGTEST679410`/prior session's data)**: real UI, `next build && next start`, real login → Test holding → OWNER company → สำนักงานใหญ่ → /product → เพิ่ม → uploaded `google_logo.png` (main) + `line_logo.png` (gallery) via real `<input type=file>` + `DataTransfer` + `change` event → both rendered as real decoded images live (`naturalWidth/naturalHeight` 128×128 / 256×144, not 0×0) → saved (`POST /api/product → 201`) → **reloaded the page** (fresh JS memory, no leftover blob cache) → re-opened via search → still rendered correctly. Confirmed via own MongoDB query (`docker exec mongodb mongosh` against the **local Docker `mongodb` container, db `appdb`** — not the Atlas `dev` cluster listed in `backend/bootstrap.json`; the running `mainapi` container actually mounts `backend/bootstrap.local.json`, which points at `mongodb://mongodb:27017/?replicaSet=rs0` — this tripped up my first two query attempts, worth remembering for next time) that `products.imageuri`/`images[]` genuinely persisted. Confirmed via direct authenticated `curl` that the S3 file is genuinely retrievable (`200`, `image/png`, byte-identical 7042-byte size) and that the same URL with no `Authorization` header still gets a clean `401` — the root-cause claim (browser can't attach a header to `<img>`) is real and the fix genuinely closes it.
- **Independently re-confirmed the report's own flagged gap**: the product LIST/search endpoint (`GET /api/product?q=...`, PG-backed, `"_source":"pgsql"`) genuinely omits `imageuri`/`images` in its projection — reproduced live: list-row thumbnail correctly falls back to the no-image icon (not a regression, `item.imageuri` really is `undefined` from that source), and opening "แก้ไข" on a list-search-selected product shows an empty Media tab (no `GET /api/product/:code` detail fetch fires at all — the edit form hydrates straight from the thin list record) even though the real saved Mongo record has the image, confirmed via a direct `fetch` reproducing the app's own authenticated GET. Pre-existing, unrelated to this diff, correctly left unfixed by the implementer.
- **NEW genuine bug found (pre-existing, NOT part of this diff, NOT fixed — flagged per Dissent rule): barcode image uploads never persist.** Created a barcode (`8901234567907`) via `/productbarcode` → เพิ่ม, uploaded an image in the "ภาพและสี" tab — it rendered correctly live (real blob, `naturalWidth/naturalHeight` 128×128, proving `AuthenticatedImg`/the upload endpoint both work correctly in isolation) — but after `POST /api/product-barcode → 201` **and** a follow-up `PUT` (edit, uploaded a second time, staying on the same tab, no tab-switch this time to rule out a tab-unmount theory), `imageuri`/`images` on the saved record were **still empty** both via the app's own authenticated `GET /api/product-barcode/:guid` and via a direct MongoDB read. Confirmed this is not specific to my test data: `db.productbarcodes.findOne({imageuri:{$ne:""}})` against the real local Mongo returns **null — zero barcodes in the entire database have ever had a persisted image**, across every prior session's sample/test data too. Traced the wiring (not fixed): `barcode-form.tsx`'s upload handler correctly does `onChange((c) => ({...c, imageuri: result.data!.url}))` on the shared form `value`, and `product-barcode-screen.tsx`'s `saveEditor` correctly forwards the whole `value` object into `createBarcode`/`updateBarcode` (`frontend/src/lib/product-barcode/api.ts`, which POSTs `{backendUrl, data}` including `data.imageuri`) — so the drop happens somewhere in the Next.js proxy route or the Go backend's create/update handler for `productbarcode`, not in the frontend form/display layer this task touched. Out of scope for this fix (a display-auth bug, not a save-payload bug) — flagging for a separate task.
- **Regression test gap closed for barcode**: `product-crud.spec.ts` already had an image-display assertion (added by the prior verification pass); `product-barcode-crud.spec.ts` had none. Added one mirroring it — uploads a real file in the "ภาพและสี" tab, asserts a `img[src^='blob:']` becomes visible with `naturalWidth > 0` (covers the live in-session `AuthenticatedImg` mechanism only, explicitly does NOT assert persistence-after-reload since that is blocked by the newly-found save bug above, documented in an inline comment referencing it). Ran all 3 specs together (3 workers) — **all pass**, including the new assertion.
- **Final sanity**: `node node_modules/typescript/bin/tsc --noEmit` — clean, exit 0 (both before and after the test-file edit).
- **Cleanup**: deleted my own test product (`VERIFY907`) and barcode (`8901234567907`) via the app's own authenticated DELETE endpoints (200/200) — confirmed soft-deleted (`deletedat`/`deletedby` set), matching the app's existing delete convention, no orphaned visible test data left behind.

## 2026-07-08 (cont.) — My own second-independent re-check of both flagged bugs above (different method, real production-shaped record, not reusing the report's own test data) — both confirmed real
- **Correcting my own earlier report to Jead**: I had told Jead `IMGTEST679410` correctly showed the "ไม่มีรูปภาพ" fallback because it genuinely had no image — that was **wrong**. Direct `GET /api/product/IMGTEST679410` just now returns `imageuri: "/goapi/s3/file/test/products/20260708_003157_bc48d45e3daa1d43.png"` and 1 gallery image — the record **does** have a saved image. What I actually saw was exactly the list-projection bug logged above: I opened that product via list search (which lacks `imageuri`), so the edit form hydrated without it. Not a false alarm — a real, reproduced instance of the already-logged bug.
- **Barcode persistence bug, re-confirmed with a fresh method**: used a real existing barcode (`8859GREEN01`, not a throwaway test record) — `GET` its full detail via the app's own authenticated endpoint (`imageuri: ""`, confirming the record itself has no image, not just the list view), then `PUT` back the same record with `imageuri` set to a fresh test path (`.../PERSIST_TEST_<ts>.png`) plus a valid `names` array (the existing record had `names: []`, which the backend's own validator rejects with "names must contain at least 1 item" — a separate, minor, pre-existing data-quality issue, not investigated further). Response: `201`, `success: true`. Immediate re-`GET`: `imageuri` back to `""` — the value was silently accepted and dropped, not merely rejected.
- **Traced the Go write path as far as static reading gets you, root cause NOT fully pinned down (being honest rather than guessing)**: confirmed the Next.js proxy (`frontend/src/app/api/product-barcode/[[...barcodePath]]/route.ts`) forwards the JSON body verbatim (no field allowlist/stripping) — ruling out the frontend proxy layer entirely. On the Go side, read `UpdateProductBarcode` (`productbarcode_http.go:169`, plain `json.Unmarshal` into `ProductBarcodeRequest`) → `svc.UpdateProductBarcode` (`productbarcode_http_service.go:283`, `docData.ProductBarcode = docReq.ToProductBarcode()`) → `ProductBarcodeRequest.ToProductBarcode()` (`product_barcode_request.go:31`, copies `ProductBarcodeBase` wholesale) → generic `CrudRepository[T].Update` (`internal/repositories/crud_repository.go:97`, whole-doc `$set` via `pst.UpdateOne`) → `PersisterMongo.toDoc` (`persister_mongo.go:168`, plain `bson.Marshal`/`Unmarshal` round-trip). Every struct in this chain has `ImageURI string` correctly tagged `json:"imageuri" bson:"imageuri"` and the embedding chain (`ProductBarcodeBase` → `ProductBarcode` → `ProductBarcodeInfo` → `ProductBarcodeData` → `ProductBarcodeDoc`, all `bson:"inline"`) looks structurally sound on paper — did not find the exact line that drops it, and did not want to guess/fabricate one (Rule 7). Since the generic `Update`/`toDoc` path is shared by every other entity in the codebase and those work fine elsewhere, the bug is most likely something field- or barcode-module-specific between the binding and the repo call that a static read didn't surface (would need either a debug breakpoint/log or a fresh pair of eyes) — a real follow-up task, not something to keep digging into solo without Jead's go-ahead given it is fully out of scope for the display-auth fix this session was about.
- Told Jead both findings plainly, asked whether to open a dedicated fix task now or defer — not proceeding into new backend work without that decision (Rule 4 scope discipline).

## 2026-07-08 (cont.) — Seeded a large multi-level Product Category (จัดหมวดสินค้า/สินค้าในหมวด) tree in DEV (holdingcode "test"), real UI + real API proxy, 16 nodes across 2 group-number slots, 3 leaf categories bound with real barcodes
- **Screens/collection**: `/productcategorygroupselectscreen` (tree create/edit) + `/productcategorylist` (browse + bind products), both `basePath "/product/category"`, backend Mongo collection `productcategories`. Config `frontend/src/lib/system-setting-screens.ts:1343-1412`. This is the separate หมวดสินค้า<->สินค้า reverse-relationship tree (ParentGUID/ParentGUIDAll/XSorts + per-node `codelist[]`) — NOT `productgroup` and NOT `categoryproductmaster` (หมวดคุณลักษณะสินค้า, `/aicloud/category`), neither of which was touched.
- **Method**: real dev-test-login -> holding "Test"(test) -> company OWNER -> สำนักงานใหญ่, via the actual Next.js preview browser (Claude_Preview MCP tool), driving the real UI buttons (เพิ่มหมวดหลัก/เพิ่มหมวดย่อย/บันทึก) for every node create, PLUS the app's own authenticated fetch (bearer token + x-bc-backend-url read from localStorage.bc_auth) hitting the exact same /api/system-settings/productcategorygroupselectscreen and /api/product-barcode/list proxy routes the UI itself calls, used only for read-verification (GET list per group-number) and for discovering real existing barcodes — no direct MongoDB/PostgreSQL writes anywhere, per dev-data-seeder convention.
- **Gotcha hit + worked around (worth remembering for next agent doing bulk UI automation here)**: this app keeps every opened tab's content mounted in the DOM simultaneously (role=tabpanel, inactive ones display:none, kept alive not unmounted) AND something in the app (observed, not root-caused — possibly a "ใช้ประจำ" frequently-used-tab auto-restore) can silently switch the active tab out from under a running automation between actions. A rapid-fire scripted sequence of เพิ่มหมวดหลัก/บันทึก clicks silently no-op'd (button found but offsetParent===null, hidden tab) until this was guarded: re-check the active [role=tab] text matches the target screen and re-click it before every button interaction, and always filter querySelectorAll button/row matches by offsetParent !== null before clicking. Also: newly-created child categories do NOT auto-expand their parent row (expandedNodes state defaults collapsed and is untouched by save) — must explicitly click the parent's "ลูก N" toggle button (aria-label starts with แสดง/ซ่อน to tell current state) to reveal a just-added child before it can be selected/clicked again.
