- **Tree shape had already changed since the last logged entry above**: this same session, before any of my own edits, live API read confirmed กาแฟ/กาแฟสำเร็จรูป/น้ำอัดลม are now flat **siblings** under เครื่องดื่ม (`3GCWUquJgFZa8zwDL9szORVrkaA`), xorder 1/2/3 respectively — NOT the 3-level-deep `เครื่องดื่ม -> กาแฟ -> กาแฟสำเร็จรูป` shape the two verification passes above recorded (last confirmed line 742, same day). No agent session logged a reparent in between, so this was very likely an unlogged manual drag by Jead himself — meaning the drag-to-reparent gesture (`moveCategoryAsChild`/`moveCategoryToRoot`, `product-category-tree-view.tsx:910-1017`) does work for him; the part that then failed was the final same-parent sibling **reorder**.
- **Reproduced live with a real pointer press-move-release** (claude-in-chrome `computer` tool, `left_click_drag` with a real 40px vertical delta) dragging น้ำอัดลม to before กาแฟสำเร็จรูป: worked correctly end-to-end — `PUT /api/system-settings/productcategorygroupselectscreen/xsort?holdingcode=test` → 201, backend route `PUT /product/category/xsort` (basePath from `frontend/src/lib/system-setting-screens.ts:1349`), and a follow-up `GET` confirmed real MongoDB `xsorts` now กาแฟ=1, น้ำอัดลม=2, กาแฟสำเร็จรูป=3 — **exactly the order Jead asked for**. Left this change in place (it's the requested state, not a test artifact).
- **Root cause of the reported failure — not a backend/payload bug, a silent-failure gap in the pointer-drag threshold**: `updatePointerDrag` (`product-category-tree-view.tsx:1145-1163`) only flips `started:true` once `Math.hypot(dx,dy) > 5`; `finishPointerDrag` (`:1198-1253`) returns at line 1210 (`if (!activeDrag.started) return;`) **before** calling `preventDefault`/`stopPropagation` and **before** setting `ignoreNextClickRef` — so on any release under the 5px threshold there is zero drag overlay, zero drop-indicator, and the browser's native `click` fires normally afterward, hitting the row's own `onClick` (`:1430-1443`), which **opens the row's edit panel instead of reordering**, with no error/toast of any kind. Reproduced live: a 2px drag on กาแฟสำเร็จรูป fired no `xsort` request, order stayed unchanged, and the edit form for that row opened instead (`GET .../productcategorygroupselectscreen/{guid}` fired, edit fields visible via `read_page`). A real user whose drag gesture doesn't clear ~5px before release (fast flick, trackpad tap-drag, or releasing just as the drop indicator would appear) sees nothing happen and, confusingly, an edit panel pop open — which reads exactly as "dragging doesn't work."
- **Not fixed per task instructions (diagnosis only)**. Plausible minimal fix for a future task: in `finishPointerDrag`, still call `preventDefault`/`stopPropagation` + set `ignoreNextClickRef` whenever a pointer-capture drag session existed at all (regardless of `started`), so a failed/aborted drag never falls through to the row's click handler; optionally surface a brief `reorderError`-style hint ("ลากไม่ถึงตำแหน่ง ลองลากให้ไกลกว่านี้") when a drag starts but resolves to no valid target.
- Did not touch backend code, did not fix the frontend, did not commit/push. Auth/session used real dev test-login (holding "test" -> company OWNER -> สำนักงานใหญ่) via claude-in-chrome MCP, not Playwright.
- Did not commit or push anything. Did not touch anything under `scopeofwork/`.

## 2026-07-08 (cont.) — Sanity-check of the above diagnosis's proposed fix: REJECTED as unsafe, no code change applied; verdict = data already fixed, not a code bug
- **Re-derived the diagnosis's own fix sketch to its logical conclusion and it breaks core UI**: `handlePointerDown` (`product-category-tree-view.tsx:1287-1313`) starts a pointer-capture drag session (sets `pointerDragRef.current` with `started:false`) on **every** `pointerdown` on any draggable row — that's also exactly what happens on an ordinary click (mouse down + up with ~0px movement). The row's `onClick` (`:1430-1443`) only opens the edit panel when `ignoreNextClickRef.current` is `false` (checked/consumed at `:1431-1436`), and that ref is only ever set `true` inside `finishPointerDrag`'s `started === true` branch (`:1214`, gated by the `if (!activeDrag.started) return;` guard at `:1210`). The diagnosis's suggested fix — set `ignoreNextClickRef`/call `preventDefault` "whenever a pointer-capture drag session existed at all (regardless of `started`)" — would therefore fire on **every single plain click** on every row (not just failed short-drags), permanently suppressing the click-to-open-edit-panel behavior for the entire tree screen. Confirmed by reading `ignoreNextClickRef` at all 6 of its usage sites (`:199,310,1214,1216,1431,1432`) — there is no other signal available at that point to distinguish "aborted drag" from "ordinary click"; both produce an identical `pointerdown -> <5px move -> pointerup` sequence with `started` staying `false`. This is not a fixable gap without a different UX mechanism (e.g. a dedicated drag-handle, a long-press-to-arm gesture, or a visible "grabbing" affordance) — none of which is a minimal fix, and none was requested.
- **Independently confirmed the reorder mechanism itself is not broken**: `node node_modules/typescript/bin/tsc --noEmit` clean (baseline, no edits made); `git status --porcelain` on the file shows no diff. Re-queried MongoDB directly (`docker exec mongodb mongosh appdb`, read-only) for the 3 sibling nodes under เครื่องดื่ม: **กาแฟ.xorder=1, น้ำอัดลม.xorder=2, กาแฟสำเร็จรูป.xorder=3** — the exact order Jead asked for, still in place from the diagnosis agent's earlier live drag test.
- **Verdict: NOT a code bug.** The 5px movement threshold before a pointer session counts as a "drag" (vs. a plain click) is standard, expected click/drag disambiguation present in effectively every custom pointer-based DnD implementation (same category of trade-off as dnd-kit's/sortable.js's own activation-distance settings) — a real, deliberate drag of a full row-height (~40px, as already live-tested and confirmed 201+DB-persisted) clears it easily. The actual root cause of Jead's report was **stale tree data**, which the diagnosis agent already corrected live via a real UI drag (data-only fix, already applied, verified still intact above) — not a defect in the JS event wiring, the xsort payload, or the backend handler. No source file was modified.
- Files touched: none (`frontend/src/app/system-settings/product-category-tree-view.tsx` unchanged). No rebuild/restart needed since nothing changed; `tsc --noEmit` re-confirmed clean as the baseline check.
- Did not commit or push anything. Did not touch anything under `scopeofwork/`.

## 2026-07-08 (cont.) — Independent re-verification of the sanity-check above (fresh browser session, real claude-in-chrome pointer drags, own MongoDB reads): PASS, plus a NEW real precision finding + closed regression-coverage gap
- **Fresh session, real gesture, own evidence**: cleared `localStorage`/`sessionStorage`, redid the full dev test-login (holding "Test" -> OWNER -> สำนักงานใหญ่) via `claude-in-chrome`, navigated to `/productcategorygroupselectscreen` group 1. Confirmed the current tree shape directly via MongoDB before touching anything: กาแฟ.xorder=1, น้ำอัดลม.xorder=2, กาแฟสำเร็จรูป.xorder=3, all siblings under เครื่องดื่ม — matches the state the two prior sessions above left in place.
- **Performed the actual drag myself** (`computer` `left_click_drag`, real pointerdown->move->up, not a synthetic click): first perturbed the order (dragged กาแฟสำเร็จรูป before น้ำอัดลม, confirmed via MongoDB: กาแฟ=1/กาแฟสำเร็จรูป=2/น้ำอัดลม=3, real `PUT .../xsort` 201), then performed the exact gesture Jead asked for (dragged น้ำอัดลม before กาแฟสำเร็จรูป) — network capture showed a real `PUT /api/system-settings/productcategorygroupselectscreen/xsort?holdingcode=test` -> **201**, and a fresh MongoDB read confirmed กาแฟ=1/น้ำอัดลม=2/กาแฟสำเร็จรูป=3. **Reloaded the page from scratch** (full navigate, not SPA state) and the real UI displayed the same correct order. **Verdict: CONFIRMED PASS**, independent of the prior sessions' own drag test.
- **New real finding beyond what the diagnosis/sanity-check sessions logged**: while probing for a "different pair" regression case, repeatedly found that a real, "started" (>5px) drag landing within a few px of a **parent-row/first-child-row boundary or an expanded-parent's-last-child/next-root-sibling boundary** can silently resolve to the WRONG operation — e.g. dragging a root category down toward the next root's top edge instead appended it as an extra CHILD of the PREVIOUS parent (`reorderCategory`'s cross-parent "insert after target" path firing against the wrong target row), and dragging a middle child up toward its parent's own row caused it to exit to ROOT level entirely (matched the parent row's own "after" zone among ROOT siblings instead of the intended child's "before" zone). Reproduced 3 times independently (once with rough screenshot-pixel targeting, twice more even after computing exact CSS `getBoundingClientRect()`-based targets via `innerHeight/screenshotHeight` DPR calibration, i.e. not just my own aiming error) — each time the request still succeeded (201) and the DB genuinely updated, so it is not a broken/silent-no-op case, just a **different, unintended, real mutation**. Each accidental mutation was independently confirmed via MongoDB and cleanly undone via further real drags (verified via MongoDB after each step), ending in the exact original tree shape (roots เครื่องดื่ม=1/ของใช้ในบ้าน=2/เครื่องเขียน=3; เครื่องดื่ม children กาแฟ=1/น้ำอัดลม=2/กาแฟสำเร็จรูป=3; ของใช้ในบ้าน children ผลิตภัณฑ์ทำความสะอาด=1/ของใช้ในครัว=2; all `parentguid`/`parentguidall` clean, no orphans) — confirmed via a final MongoDB query plus a hard page reload. **Not fixed** (same "diagnosis only" scope as this whole investigation thread; a real UX robustness gap for a future task — the drop-zone math in `getRowDropPosition`/`getPointerDropCandidate` (`product-category-tree-view.tsx:1025-1133`) doesn't leave enough margin at these seams for automated OR careless real-user drags).
- **Regression check on a genuinely different, boundary-safe pair**: swapped น้ำอัดลม/กาแฟสำเร็จรูป a second time via a fresh drag after temporarily moving น้ำอัดลม out and back in (interior-safe positions only, learned from the boundary finding above) — same real `xsort` 201 + MongoDB-confirmed reorder, proving the mechanism generalizes beyond the exact original pair, not a one-off.
- **Regression-coverage gap closed**: added a new Playwright test to `frontend/e2e/product-category-list-crud.spec.ts` ("sibling reorder via the real xsort API persists and displays in the new order"). Explicitly does NOT simulate a real pointer drag (documented inline why: Playwright's `mouse` primitives land at exact CSS px with no DPR ambiguity, but this component's live FLIP-animation preview reflow during a drag makes a scripted multi-tick drag path flaky to author reliably, and the pointer-event/threshold/preview-animation half of the flow was already verified manually above, not something this test claims to cover). Instead drives the SAME real `xsort` PUT `saveXSorts` calls (`{guidfixed, code, xorder}[]` payload) against 3 freshly-created sibling categories (group 19, same disposable-regression-slot convention as the existing tests in this file), verifies the reorder via a fresh API read, then verifies via a hard page reload that the real UI displays the new order too. Ran the new test alone (pass, 30s) then the full 4-test file together (all 4 pass, 1.6m). **Covered**: xsort payload shape, backend persistence, fresh-read correctness, UI-after-reload correctness. **NOT covered**: the actual pointer-drag gesture, 5px start threshold, FLIP preview animation, or the boundary-precision finding above — noted in the test's own header comment so a future reader doesn't assume drag-level coverage exists.
- `node node_modules/typescript/bin/tsc --noEmit` — clean, exit 0. `git status --porcelain -- frontend/src/app/system-settings/product-category-tree-view.tsx` — empty (source untouched, matching the "no code fix" conclusion). Only `frontend/e2e/product-category-list-crud.spec.ts` has a diff.
- Did not commit or push anything. Did not touch anything under `scopeofwork/`.

## 2026-07-08 (cont.) — Root cause PINNED for the boundary-drop bug (line 767 above): two distinct, independently-reproduced defects in `getPointerDropCandidate`, both stemming from trusting live `document.elementFromPoint` hit-testing that the drag itself corrupts. Diagnosis only, no source edited (per task scope).
- **Method**: real `claude-in-chrome` `computer` `left_click_drag` gestures (multi-step press->move->release, not synthetic) against the live เครื่องดื่ม/กาแฟ/น้ำอัดลม/กาแฟสำเร็จรูป/ของใช้ในบ้าน/เครื่องเขียน tree (group 1, re-verified live via API before touching anything — matched line 767's end state exactly). Instrumented via a temporary in-page monkeypatch of `document.elementFromPoint` (logs x/y, returned element, `closest('[data-category-row-guid]')`, `closest('[data-category-child-drop-guid]')`, `closest("[data-category-root-drop='true']")`, and live row/chip rects) — this is the "add temporary instrumentation to see real internal values" allowance from the task, not guesswork; every number below is a captured log entry or a fresh API read. A separate attempt to pause mid-drag via synthetic `PointerEvent` dispatch (after stubbing `setPointerCapture`, which real un-trusted dispatch always throws on: `NotFoundError: Failed to execute 'setPointerCapture'...`) proved the two defects are **timing-dependent on real per-frame React commits** — a same-tick synthetic dispatch loop with no `rAF`/paint gap between moves did NOT reproduce either defect (DOM never re-rendered between calls), confirming why the task said this only reproduces with a real, time-spaced drag.
- **Defect A (dominant — exactly reproduces "moved out to root level"), `product-category-tree-view.tsx:1121-1133` + `1070-1119`**: the dragged row itself gets `pointer-events-none` while dragging (`isDragging && "pointer-events-none ..."`, line 1415-1416) so `document.elementFromPoint` (called at line 1054, used again at 1121) sees straight through it to its own non-data-bearing wrapper `<div key={node.detail.guidfixed} className="grid w-full">` (line 1407, no `data-category-row-guid`) — for ANY `clientY` still within the dragged row's own original rect span (which is most of a short/modest drag, since that's where the pointer starts and stays for a while). `element.closest('[data-category-row-guid]')` then finds nothing (`rowElement` is null), so line 1123 falls back to `findNearestRowCandidate()` (1070-1119), which explicitly **excludes the dragged node itself** via `canDropOnTarget`'s self-guid check (line 1040) and picks whichever OTHER row is nearest by raw `rect.top`/`rect.bottom` distance (lines 1093-1099, strict `<` tie-break = first in DOM order wins). Because the dragged row's own slot is a dead zone that's skipped, this raw-distance search can jump past the dragged node's real siblings straight to an unrelated row (e.g. the next ROOT sibling), with no relation to how far the user actually moved the pointer.
  - **Reproduced live**: dragged "น้ำอัดลม" (child of เครื่องดื่ม, last position, row span y=242-287) only 26px down (264→290 in CSS px, confirmed the tool's coordinate space = CSS px via a calibration click) — pointer never visually left the item's own 45px-tall row, let alone approached "ของใช้ในบ้าน" (next root sibling, starts at y=287). Logged: every `elementFromPoint` call in that span returned `row:null` (fell through the ghosted source row to its wrapper) for `y=272` and `y=277`; `findNearestRowCandidate` then computed distance-to-"กาแฟสำเร็จรูป"(prev sibling, bottom=242)=35 vs distance-to-"ของใช้ในบ้าน"(next root, top=287)=10 at y=277, so "ของใช้ในบ้าน" won, `getRowDropPosition(277, ของใช้ในบ้านRow)` returned `"before"` (topZone=300.16). Result: `reorderCategory` fired with `targetParentGuid=""`, `movingAcrossParents=true`. **Confirmed via a fresh API read after the drag**: `น้ำอัดลม.parentguid` changed from เครื่องดื่ม's guid to `""` (root), `xorder=2`, wedged between เครื่องดื่ม(1) and ของใช้ในบ้าน(now 3)/เครื่องเขียน(now 4) — a real, committed, silent reparent-to-root from a drag that never visually left the item's own family.
- **Defect B (reproduces "reparented into previous parent" / inverted order near a parent/first-child boundary), `product-category-tree-view.tsx:1025-1032` (zone math) vs `1399-1401`+`1464-1470`+`1530-1542` (drag-only affordances)**: `canAcceptChildDrop` (line 1399-1401, depends on `dragState` so it only exists WHILE dragging) renders an inline "วางเป็นลูก" chip (`data-category-child-drop-guid`, lines 1530-1542) inside every eligible row's flex-wrap content, and a separate centered "วางเข้าเป็นลูกของหมวดนี้" overlay (lines 1464-1470, `top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2`, NOT confined to the 44% "inside" band `getRowDropPosition` computes at lines 1025-1032). Both are absent before the drag starts, so a row's rendered height/content genuinely changes ONLY once dragging begins — confirmed live: "กาแฟสำเร็จรูป"'s row measured 197-242 (height 45) before any drag, then measured **199-246 (height 47)** mid-drag once its "วางเป็นลูก" chip appeared and wrapped to a 2nd line (chip's own rect: top 205.5-238.5). At `y=206` — which by the row's OWN live (grown) topZone (212.16) should still be `"before"`, and by the pre-drag static topZone (209.6) definitely should be `"before"` — `document.elementFromPoint(239,206)` instead resolved to the "วางเข้าเป็นลูกของหมวดนี้" overlay DIV (its centered, non-band-confined layout physically covers that pixel), short-circuiting straight to `position:"inside"` before the row's own 28%-band math is ever consulted for that click point.
  - **Reproduced live**: dragged "น้ำอัดลม" up toward the เครื่องดื่ม/กาแฟ boundary (aiming to move it to the FRONT of its siblings); final resolved point (y=138) landed inside เครื่องดื่ม's own "inside" band (contaminated the same way — its content grew from 47px static to still-47 but the "inside" indicator similarly overlapped the "after" 28% zone), computing `moveCategoryAsChild(น้ำอัดลม, เครื่องดื่ม)`. Since `moveCategoryAsChild` always **appends to the END** of the target's children (line 934: `nextOrder = nextChildren.length + 1`), the result was the exact OPPOSITE of the user's upward-dragging intent: น้ำอัดลม moved from position 2 to position 3 (LAST). Confirmed via a fresh API read: กาแฟ=1, กาแฟสำเร็จรูป=2, น้ำอัดลม=3 immediately after the drag (was กาแฟ=1, น้ำอัดลม=2, กาแฟสำเร็จรูป=3 before).
- **Common root cause (both defects)**: `getPointerDropCandidate` (1053-1133) resolves "which row/zone is under the pointer" via **live** `document.elementFromPoint` + DOM `closest()`, which is contaminated by the drag's own visual side effects — the dragged row's `pointer-events-none` ghosting (Defect A) and the drag-only conditionally-rendered, non-band-confined "inside"/child-drop affordances (Defect B) — instead of resolving purely from a **static, precomputed row-geometry model** that's independent of anything the drag itself renders.
- **Fix proposal (not applied — diagnosis-only scope)**: snapshot every row's rect ONCE at drag start (`handlePointerDown`, before any drag-only content exists, reusing the same `Map<guid, DOMRect>` pattern the file already has for `pendingLayoutRectsRef`/`captureTreeLayout()`), **including the dragged row's own rect** (do not exclude it from the geometry, only from being a valid *drop target* via the existing `canDropOnTarget` self-guid check). Rewrite `getPointerDropCandidate` to resolve `clientY` against this frozen, sorted-by-`top` snapshot with a simple band lookup (falls to the nearer of the two immediate tree-neighbors only when `clientY` lands inside the dragged row's own excluded band — which, in the frozen list, is always the row's true previous/next sibling, not an arbitrary tree-wide nearest-by-distance pick) instead of calling `document.elementFromPoint`/`closest()` for row identification. Keep `getRowDropPosition`'s 28/44/28 percent-band formula unchanged (line 1025-1032) — apply it to the frozen rect, not a live one. The `data-category-child-drop-guid` chip and `data-category-root-drop` bar can stay as pure visual affordances but should stop being the PRIMARY signal (drop the `elementFromPoint`-based priority checks at lines 1058-1068); root-drop should trigger only when `clientY` is past the last frozen row's `bottom`. This touches only the row/zone-resolution helpers (~1025-1133) — `reorderCategory`/`moveCategoryAsChild`/`moveCategoryToRoot` (816-1017) are correct given a correct target, so untouched. Sanity-checked against currently-working cases in this same worklog thread (mid-list sibling reorder, cross-parent reparent via "inside" zone, both confirmed working at lines 750-751/766) — both resolve via the same frozen-band lookup once `clientY` is outside the dragged row's own band, so the fix doesn't change their behavior; the already-diagnosed separate 5px-threshold/short-drag issue (line 752-761) is untouched (different code path, `updatePointerDrag`'s `distance > 5` gate).
- **Restored the tree to its exact pre-task shape** after both live reproductions, via the app's own authenticated `PUT` endpoints (record reparent + `xsort`, same calls the UI itself makes — explicitly permitted by the task instructions for restoration) rather than more drags, since the exact target state was already known precisely: เครื่องดื่ม(root,1) children กาแฟ=1/น้ำอัดลม=2/กาแฟสำเร็จรูป=3; roots เครื่องดื่ม=1/ของใช้ในบ้าน=2/เครื่องเขียน=3. Verified via a fresh API read (exact match) and a hard page reload (clean UI, no stuck drag state).

## 2026-07-08 (cont.) — Fix APPLIED to `product-group-tree-view.tsx` (`/productgroup`): ported the sibling category-tree screen's proven `getPointerDropCandidate` rewrite, adapted (not copy-pasted) to this file's own naming/refs. Build clean, live sanity drags pass, tree restored.
- **Independently re-verified the diagnosis above against the real current source before touching anything**: confirmed `getPointerDropCandidate` (`product-group-tree-view.tsx:920-1000`, pre-fix) still used `document.elementFromPoint`/`closest()` exactly as the diagnosis described, and confirmed the sibling `product-category-tree-view.tsx` has an **uncommitted** local fix already applied (`git status --porcelain` showed it modified, `git diff` read in full) — this is the real, already-working reference implementation, not a hypothetical. Note: the *actual* applied sibling fix is NOT a "frozen-`getBoundingClientRect()`-snapshot-at-drag-start" model as both the diagnosis's and the earlier session's fix proposals described in prose — it's simpler: `getPointerDropCandidate` queries **live** `root.querySelectorAll("[data-category-row-guid]")` on every call (no caching across the drag) but resolves the target purely by **rect-containment** (`clientY >= rect.top && clientY <= rect.bottom`) against each row's own live rect, never via `document.elementFromPoint`/`closest()`. The critical anti-jiggle guard: if the row whose rect *contains* `clientY` is the dragged row itself, return `null` (no target) outright — never falls through to a nearest-row scan in that case, which is exactly what prevented Defect-A's "unrelated row mutates" failure mode. Re-derived this from reading the real diff, not assumed from the diagnosis's textual description.
- **Applied the adapted fix** to `product-group-tree-view.tsx`: replaced `getPointerDropCandidate` (was lines 920-1000) with the same rect-containment/`containingRow`-vs-`nearestRow` logic, renamed to this file's own identifiers (`groupTreeLookupsRef`, `data-group-root-drop`, `data-group-row-guid`, `data-group-row-guid` dataset key) — confirmed all these refs/attributes already exist unchanged elsewhere in the file before wiring the new function to them. Also removed the now-dead `data-group-child-drop-guid={node.detail.guidfixed}` attribute from the "วางเป็นลูก" chip (kept the chip itself as a pure visual affordance, matching the sibling fix — it's no longer a `elementFromPoint`/`closest()` hit-test target since the new resolver never calls either). Did not touch `moveGroupAsChild`/`reorderGroup`/`moveGroupToRoot`/`canDropOnTarget`/`getRowDropPosition` — the fix only changes how `{guid, position}` gets resolved, not what happens once resolved, same reasoning the diagnosis's own re-confirmation already established.
- **Build/typecheck clean**: `node node_modules/typescript/bin/tsc --noEmit` — 0 errors. `node node_modules/next/dist/bin/next build` — compiled successfully, typecheck passed, all 31 static pages generated, no warnings tied to this file.

  1. **Re-ran the exact Defect-A repro from the diagnosis** — 13px in-place jiggle-drag on "กาแฟและชา" ((300,257)->(300,270)), real `left_click_drag`. Result: **no-op**, confirmed via `read_network_requests` (zero new PUT calls fired) and a screenshot (tree pixel-identical to before). Previously this exact class of gesture silently reparented the unrelated "ขนมขบเคี้ยว" row into เครื่องดื่ม — now fixed.
  2. **Reparent-via-drag sanity check** (the screen's only working nesting path, per task instructions to not break it): dragged "ขนมขบเคี้ยว" ((300,297)->(300,174)) onto เครื่องดื่ม's inside/middle zone. Result: **worked correctly** — เครื่องดื่ม's badge went 2->3 children, อาหารและเครื่องดื่ม's badge went 3->2 (lost a direct child), two real `PUT` calls fired (`.../productgroup/{guid}` and `.../productgroup/xsort`, both **201**). `moveGroupAsChild` confirmed still functioning through the new resolver.

Repeated with a deliberately deep/aggressive drag ("อาหารแห้ง" dropped squarely onto ขนมขบเคี้ยว's own row, itself now nested inside เครื่องดื่ม) — correctly created a 3-level-deep nest, again verified via fresh API read. 3 independent successful reparents total this session, 0 misfires.

- **Verdict: PASS.** The fix holds under independent adversarial re-testing (jiggle no-op, boundary-precision sibling reorder, 3x reparent including a 3-level-deep nest), and the tree was left exactly as found.
- **Separate bug found + documented, NOT fixed (out of scope for this drop-zone-precision task)**: the "เพิ่มกลุ่มย่อย" (Add Subgroup) button on this screen silently creates the new group as an orphaned ROOT instead of nesting it under the selected parent, with no error shown. Root cause confirmed by reading source, not guessed: `handleOpenGroupCreate` (`system-settings-screen.tsx:1743`) sets `initialForm.parentguid = parentGuid` correctly (confirmed via DOM inspect — the "กลุ่มแม่" field's live `value` really does hold the parent guid), but `buildPayload` (`system-settings-screen.tsx:15667`, `if (field.readOnly) continue;`) skips every field marked `readOnly` in its config, and `productgroup`'s `parentguid` field IS configured `readOnly: true` (`system-setting-screens.ts:1338`). The category screen has a dedicated slug-specific block (`system-settings-screen.tsx:1876`, gated on `slug === "productcategorygroupselectscreen" || "productcategorylist"`) that re-adds `parentguid`/`parentguidall`/an auto-computed `xsorts` to the payload AFTER `buildPayload` strips them — that block does NOT cover `productgroup`, so for this screen `parentguid` is simply never sent on create. Live-reproduced: created "ZZZ-DEBUG-CHILD-TEST" via the real button after selecting a parent row, fresh API read showed `parentguid: "", parentguidall: ""`. This is NOT new — the live dev dataset already contained a row literally named `[VERIFY-BUG-เพิ่มกลุ่มย่อย ห้ามใช้ orphan]` from an earlier session flagging the exact same defect, and a second pre-existing orphan `[ซ้ำ-ห้ามใช้] เครื่องเขียนและอุปกรณ์สำนักงาน (orphan, ลบไม่ได้)`. Confirmed via source read that this is a real, separate, already-known bug, not a misunderstanding on my part.
- **Also found**: `DELETE /product/group/:id` (`productgroup_http_service.go:239-242`, `existsGroupRefInProduct(holdingCode, []string{findDoc.Code})`) refuses to delete ANY group whose own `code` is `""` with `group code "" is referenced in product barcode` — because the empty-string code check matches broadly against product barcodes that also have no group assigned, a false positive for the common case of a group created with no explicit `code`. This is why the dataset's existing orphan rows are marked "ลบไม่ได้" (cannot delete) and why my own test/debug leftovers (`DEBUGROOT123`, `ZZZ-DEBUG-CHILD-TEST`, and one batch of `E2E...` roots from an earlier failed test-file draft) could not be deleted via the API either — confirmed by attempting delete on all of them and getting the identical error every time. ## 2026-07-09 — Design pass (no code yet): up/down move buttons + Undo/Redo for `/productgroup` tree
- Jead asked for an easier alternative to pixel-drag sibling reorder ("วางก่อน/วางหลัง เล่นยาก") plus session-local Undo/Redo for moves. This pass is DESIGN ONLY per the task instructions — no source edited.
- **Read the full current `product-group-tree-view.tsx` (1506 lines)** fresh (it was rewritten earlier this session to use frozen-rect drop resolution). Confirmed the 3 real move functions and their exact API calls: `reorderGroup(draggedGuid, targetGuid, position:"before"|"after", siblings)` (same-parent OR cross-parent reorder-with-position; calls `saveGroupRecord` PUT `/api/system-settings/productgroup/{guid}` only when the parent actually changes, always calls `saveXSorts` PUT `/api/system-settings/productgroup/xsort` with a `updateList` that already unions BOTH the old-parent and new-parent sibling payloads), `moveGroupAsChild(draggedGuid, targetGuid)` and `moveGroupToRoot(draggedGuid)` (reparent-only paths, same two calls). All three are only invoked from `finishPointerDrag` today.
- **Design: up/down buttons** — reuse `reorderGroup` directly, no new API/mechanism. Placed as 2 new ghost icon buttons in the existing per-row "Row actions" cluster (next to the FolderPlus/Edit3/Trash2 buttons), using the exact per-parent sibling array (`nodes`) already available as a closure var in `renderTreeNodes(nodes, level)` — up = `reorderGroup(guid, nodes[index-1].guid, "before", nodes)`, down = `reorderGroup(guid, nodes[index+1].guid, "after", nodes)`. Disabled at `index===0`/`index===nodes.length-1` and whenever the existing `canDragRows` gate (readOnly/saving/loading/search-active) is false, since `nodes` is a filtered subset during search just like drag already accounts for.
- **Design: Undo/Redo** — one new `{stack: MoveRecord[], index: number}` state (index starts -1), `MoveRecord = {guid, before: MoveSnapshot, after: MoveSnapshot}`, `MoveSnapshot = {parentGuid, parentGuidAll, xsorts}`. All 3 move functions push one record right after their save succeeds (never in a drag-vs-button-specific path, so drag moves AND button moves are both undoable automatically); `before.xsorts` = each affected guid's CURRENT xorder read from `typedGroups` captured before this move's own `setOrderOverrides`/`setParentOverrides` calls; `after.xsorts` = the same `updateList` already sent to `saveXSorts`. Undo/Redo call one shared `applyMoveSnapshot(guid, snapshot)` that re-calls the SAME real `saveGroupRecord`/`saveXSorts` (not a client-only visual revert), then moves the index pointer. History resets inside the existing `useEffect(() => {...}, [records])` that already clears other optimistic overrides on an unrelated external reload (verified `onRefresh` is wired only into this component's own error-catch branches, never called on a successful move, so this reset does not fire after every own move).
- **Advisor consult**: asked GLM (frontend domain) for a critique. Score **7/10** — real, adopted catches: Undo/Redo (and the move buttons) must also be gated off while a save/apply is in flight (not just their own busy flag), history index must start at -1, and snapshot capture must happen before this move's own optimistic setState. Several other claimed "bugs" were false alarms once checked against the source already read this session (updateList already unions old+new-parent siblings; onRefresh isn't called on move success; the sibling array is already per-parent not flat-global; xsort `code` is a hardcoded `"X"` literal, not server-derived) — logged in full in `.agents/advisor-scorecard.md`.
- **Scope note / open question for Jead**: the page-level toolbar with "เพิ่มกลุ่มหลัก"/"เพิ่มกลุ่มย่อย" actually lives in `system-settings-screen.tsx`, not in `product-group-tree-view.tsx` (confirmed via grep). Since this task's scope is `product-group-tree-view.tsx` ONLY, the Undo/Redo buttons are designed as a small in-card toolbar strip rendered by the tree-view component itself (above the scrollable list, always visible), not next to the page-level Add buttons — flagged as an open choice for the implementation pass (touching `system-settings-screen.tsx` too is a 2-line alternative if Jead prefers that placement).
- Not yet implemented: no source file was edited this pass, per the task's explicit "design only, do not write code yet" instruction.
- Also confirmed (not touched): the sibling screen `product-category-tree-view.tsx` (จัดหมวดสินค้า) has the identical drag-and-drop pattern and would benefit from the same up/down + undo/redo treatment later — explicitly out of scope for this task, follow-up for Jead to request separately.

- **Point 1 (initial shape)**: fresh API read confirmed root→3-children shape before touching anything. PASS.
- **Point 2 (up/down button reorder)**: clicked the real "ย้ายลง" (move-down) button on a middle sibling; fresh API read showed the xorder swap persisted. PASS.
- **Point 3 (Undo)**: clicked "เลิกทำ"; fresh API read showed exact revert to the pre-move order. PASS.
- **Point 4 (Redo)**: clicked "ทำซ้ำ"; fresh API read showed the move reapplied. PASS.
- **Point 5 (real reparent drag + undo)**: first drag attempt (using raw `getBoundingClientRect()` CSS-px coordinates as `computer` tool click coordinates) grabbed and reordered a completely unrelated ROOT-level row instead of the intended child — **root cause: this browser's `window.innerWidth/innerHeight` (3277×1559) is NOT the same coordinate space as the `computer`/`left_click_drag` tool's screenshot-pixel coordinates (1568×746, ratio ≈0.4785, i.e. `devicePixelRatio` 1.5 plus an extra ~1.39x factor) — CSS-px rects from `getBoundingClientRect()` must be scaled by `screenshotWidth/window.innerWidth` before use as click/drag coordinates in this environment, or clicks land on the wrong row entirely.** Cleanly undone via the Undo button and confirmed reverted via fresh API read (no lasting damage — worth recording as a durable gotcha for any future claude-in-chrome pixel-drag test in this repo). Redid the drag with correctly-scaled coordinates: real pointer drag of one child onto a sibling row's middle ("inside") band correctly reparented it — fresh API read confirmed `parentguid`/`parentguidall` updated to the new parent. Undo correctly restored the original `parentguid`/`parentguidall`/`xorder`, confirmed via fresh API read. PASS (after coordinate-scale correction).
- **Point 6 (redo cleared after a new move)**: made a move, undid it, made a DIFFERENT move; confirmed via DOM (`disabled` attribute) that Redo was correctly disabled/cleared afterward, and the new move's own order persisted via fresh API read (not the discarded redo target). PASS.
- **Point 7 (boundary disable)**: confirmed via DOM `disabled` attribute (not just faded opacity) that the first root-level sibling's up button and the last root-level sibling's down button were disabled, and re-confirmed the same for the fresh 3-child test fixture (first child's up disabled, last child's down disabled, middle child both enabled). PASS.
- **Point 8 (restore tree)**: all moves made during testing were undone/reverted and confirmed via fresh API read to exactly match the pre-test order for every pre-existing row, including the one unrelated root-level row perturbed by the coordinate-scale mistake above. The 4 new test fixtures themselves could NOT be deleted — `DELETE` returned `400 {"message":"group code \"\" is referenced in product barcode"}`, the exact same pre-existing, already-documented (2026-07-08 entry above) empty-`code` false-positive bug that also blocks deletion of every other leftover test row already in this dataset. Renamed all 4 via PUT (not blocked) to a `[ลบไม่ได้-verify-task-orphan-2026-07-09] ...` prefix, matching the dataset's established convention, so they read as known debris. Did not attempt to fix the delete bug (out of scope, already flagged).
- **Point 9 (regression coverage)**: added a third test to `frontend/e2e/product-group-tree-crud.spec.ts` — "up/down move buttons + undo/redo reorder and revert via the real xsort API" — following the existing two tests' pattern exactly (creates via "เพิ่มกลุ่มหลัก" + record-PUT nesting, reads via `page.request.get`/`put` against the real API, asserts on fresh reads not optimistic state). Covers: boundary-disable DOM check, down-button reorder + fresh-API assert, Undo + fresh-API assert, Redo + fresh-API assert. Ran standalone (`ok`, 24.1s) and as part of the full 3-test file (`3 passed`, 1.2m) via `./node_modules/.bin/playwright test e2e/product-group-tree-crud.spec.ts` (plain `npx playwright` failed with "system cannot find the path specified" in this shell — had to invoke the local `.bin` shim directly).

Confirmed the category file's earlier "frozen-geometry" fix this session was narrower than the reference's: `getPointerDropCandidate` avoids `document.elementFromPoint()`/`closest()` via rect-containment scanning, but still does a **live** `querySelectorAll` DOM read on every raw pointermove (no per-drag cache, no rAF coalescing) -- NOT the reference's one-shot-snapshot-at-drag-start model. Also confirmed the category file still has row-level `onPointerMove`/`onPointerUp`/`onPointerCancel` handlers ALONGSIDE the window-level listeners (the exact double-dispatch bug the reference already fixed), and a full `previewOrderOverrides`/`previewSiblingReorder` live-reflow-while-hovering mechanism the reference deleted entirely.
- **Group-number scoping resolved with certainty (not guessed)**: read `system-settings-screen.tsx`'s fetch logic (`loadRecords`, ~line 1270) -- for slug `productcategorygroupselectscreen`, the list fetch sets a server-side `group-number` query param whenever a group is selected, so the `records` prop this component receives is ALREADY scoped to one groupnumber before any client code runs. `typedCategories`/siblings/candidates are built purely from `records`, so reorder/reparent candidates can never cross a group-number slot -- no additional client-side guard is needed, parity with the reference (which has no such concept). The one real difference: the move/undo-redo payloads must keep setting `groupnumber: groupNumber ?? recordGroupNumber(draggedRecord)` (already present in the 3 existing move functions; must also be added to the new ported `applyMoveSnapshot`).
- **CodeList confirmed unaffected**: `codelist` lives directly on the category record (`categoryRecord.codelist`, used by `product-category-items-editor.tsx`). Every move function's save payload spreads `...draggedRecord` first then overrides only `parentguid`/`parentguidall`/`groupnumber`/`xsorts` -- `codelist` passes through untouched. This is pre-existing correct behavior, not something the port changes.
- **Concrete port plan produced** (function-level, not yet applied): (1) add ChevronUp/Redo2/Undo2 imports + `MoveSnapshot`/`MoveRecord` types + `moveHistory`/`isMoveInFlight` state + `pushMoveRecord`/`applyMoveSnapshot`/`undoMove`/`redoMove`, wire into `reorderCategory`/`moveCategoryAsChild`/`moveCategoryToRoot` (add `isMoveInFlight` guard + try/finally + `pushMoveRecord` on success, matching reference); add Undo/Redo button bar in `CardContent` (only reachable after the `groupNumber===null` early return, so `groupNumber` is always non-null there). (3) delete the entire preview mechanism (`previewOrderOverrides` state+ref, `previewDropKeyRef`, `setPreviewOrderOverridesState`, `hasPreviewOrder`, `clearDragPreview`, `previewSiblingReorder`, and all call sites in `updatePointerDrag`/`reorderCategory`/`moveCategoryAsChild`/`moveCategoryToRoot`/`finishPointerDrag`/`cancelPointerDrag`); simplify `typedCategories`' `orderOverride` back to `orderOverrides[guid]` only. (4) remove row-level `onPointerMove`/`onPointerUp`/`onPointerCancel` + their handler functions (double-dispatch fix), keep only window-level listeners. (5) port `DragGeometryRow`/`DragGeometry` types + `dragGeometryRef` + `captureDragGeometry` (using `data-category-row-guid`/`data-category-root-drop`, and a NEW `data-category-inside-drop-guid` marker needed on the "Drop child" chip span, which the category file currently lacks) + the drag-start `useLayoutEffect` snapshot-on-mount/scroll + `resolveDragPointerTarget` (chip-rect-first, then top/bottom-half split via `clientY < row.mid`, replacing the current 28/44/28-band `getRowDropPosition`) + `latestPointerRef`/`dragRafRef`/`dragGhostRef` + `handleRawPointerMove`/`processPendingDragMove`/`queueDragFrame`/`cancelPendingDragFrame`, matching the reference's synchronous pre-commit geometry snapshot at the 5px-threshold crossing. (6) drop `offsetX`/`offsetY` from `DragState`/`PointerDragState`, switch `renderDragOverlay` to a `dragGhostRef` div with imperative `translateY` writes (keep the category-only "product count" chip in the overlay). (7) add `touch-none` to the row className (currently missing). (8) extend the `[records, groupNumber]` reset effect to also reset `moveHistory`/`isMoveInFlight`/`dragGeometryRef`/`latestPointerRef`/`dragRafRef` (mirroring reference), and extend the unmount-cleanup effect to cancel `dragRafRef`.

### 2026-07-10 (cont. 1) — `/uat-crud-mongo` Product Group: fixed subgroup nesting + empty-code delete false positive; full UAT PASS
- Ran the requested UI → API → Mongo UAT on `/productgroup` with unique manual data (`...700794`) and reproduced both handoff bugs before changing source: (1) the Add Subgroup form visibly held the selected parent GUID, but Mongo persisted `parentguid: ""` / `parentguidall: ""` and rendered the new row as a root; (2) deleting that unreferenced code-less group failed with the exact UI/API error `group code "" is referenced in product barcode`, while Mongo retained the live document.
- Frontend root cause/fix: generic `buildPayload` correctly skips read-only fields, so `system-settings-screen.tsx` now has a `productgroup` save adapter that restores `parentguid`, recursively derives `parentguidall`, and initializes sibling `xsorts`. Fresh manual rerun persisted child `3GHrK0jIvaATSgANs7izuXtCmd9` under root `3GHqBiRS24BjqVhTgqteoXNPMBk`, with both parent fields equal to the root GUID and `xsorts: [{code:"X", xorder:1}]`.
- Backend root cause/fix: product-group deletion was checking `productbarcodes.groupcode` with `$in:[""]`, so every unassigned barcode was a false reference. Delete and batch-delete now check non-empty `groupguid` first, only use a trimmed non-empty code as a legacy fallback, and propagate repository errors instead of ignoring them. User-facing referenced-group errors are Thai and no longer disclose an empty technical code.
- Adjacent propagation defect fixed in the same path: the product-group Kafka consumer previously updated barcodes by `groupcode == ""`, which could rename every unassigned barcode after editing any code-less group. ### 2026-07-10 (cont. 4) — Fixed Product `Product not found`; Product/Barcode business-key projections reconciled
- Root cause confirmed from runtime, not UI guesswork: PostgreSQL `product` held 29 projection rows while active MongoDB `products` held 20 documents / 19 distinct codes, with only one code intersecting. The old rebuild synthesized Product masters from `productbarcodes`, `/product/resync` only upserted and never removed projection-only rows, the Next adapter synthesized `guidfixed=code`, and the UI rejected a successfully hydrated record when its real GUID differed from that fake value.
- Product contract/fix: Product `code` is normalized uppercase/no-whitespace and unique per Holding; immutable `guidfixed` is direct CRUD/audit identity only. Detail/delete accept normalized code fallback, generic edit keeps Product code immutable, UI list/hydration/selection uses code without inventing GUIDs, and delete remains disabled until a real Mongo detail GUID is loaded. The Mongo product index is active-partial unique on `(holdingcode,code)` plus unique `(holdingcode,guidfixed)`.
- Replaced barcode-derived Product rebuild with active Mongo `products` as the complete driving set. Reconcile upserts metadata and removes PG-only rows without truncating retained PG Product rows, preserving all balance/pending fields. `/product/resync` now performs this reconciliation before Kafka publication; explicit `usecache:false` reaches backend correctly. The older duplicate active `GREENM2` record was soft-deleted through the real API and the newer linked record was retained.
- Barcode contract/fix: barcode text may repeat across products; active `(holdingcode,itemcode,barcode)` is unique and `itemcode` is normalized Product `code` when linked. An unlinked barcode remains valid with empty `itemcode`. Mongo/PG repositories, Kafka upsert/delete, rebuild/resync, BOM/ref propagation, price history, cache scan, and relevant frontend identities now use the composite where a business relation is required; barcode-only ambiguous lookup returns an explicit error instead of choosing an arbitrary row. Regression proved same barcode under two itemcodes returns `201/201`, duplicate same composite returns `400`, then both test records were deleted.
- Final audit hardening: Product UI now clears and token-guards list/detail/editor state when Holding changes; Barcode Quick Actions use the real shelf/price/BOM routes and pass both `itemcode+barcode`; price-history/shelf screens preselect the exact composite; BOM duplicate/expansion identities, versioned `boms[].bom` propagation, bulk save/import dedupe, direct detail, price history, sale-invoice BOM pricing, and ProductBarcode ref/BOM response models retain `itemcode`. ProductBarcode PG rebuild is atomic (transaction + truncate/copy), database rebuild initialization is serialized per Holding, and projection/cache queries no longer rely on invalid GUID joins or stale SQL fields.
- DEV data/projection log: active Mongo Product `20 docs / 19 codes` → soft-delete one duplicate → `19/19`; PG Product `29` → reconcile → `19/19`, set diff `0`, all 19 detail calls returned 200, retained balances/pending values unchanged. Mongo ProductBarcode stayed `24/24` active composites; PG ProductBarcode was rebuilt from `8` to `24/24`, final composite set diff `0`. No pre-existing product-group/product-category E2E/debug/orphan data was cleaned.
- Verification: in-app `/menu` → logical `/product` shows `19/19`; selecting Product/Barcode records shows full detail with Edit enabled and neither `Product not found` nor fallback warning. Barcode `DUPCHK023401` retained `itemcode=DUPCHK`, and all three Quick Actions carried the exact composite. Final `npx playwright test e2e/product-crud.spec.ts --workers=1` PASS 1/1 (25.1s) with create/read/update/delete cleanup; frontend targeted Vitest 24/24, typecheck PASS, production build PASS; touched Go package tests PASS under Go 1.26; fast backend binary deploy healthy and `/healthz` 200; Mongo/PG Product set diff `0`, Barcode composite set diff `0`. `CODE-MAP.md` regenerated.
- Remaining explicit gaps: local ClickHouse currently has no `productbarcode` table. Rebuild reports this failure rather than silently falling back; do not create the table by hand—fix/verify backend-owned ClickHouse provisioning in a dedicated follow-up. Legacy stock/cost transaction requests still carry only `holdingcode+barcode`; duplicate barcode now fails explicitly instead of selecting the wrong product, but those transaction contracts still need `itemcode` end-to-end. Broader legacy GUID/barcode-only modules need a separate source/runtime audit. No commit or push.

### 2026-07-14 (cont. 4) — Fixed the data-model graph first-entry blank state
- Two source/runtime-confirmed causes were fixed. The BC wrapper had treated the Vite shell's opaque `no-cors` response as readiness while API `:3002` starts asynchronously; it now probes proxied `/api/graph/status`, requires `response.ok`, keeps the iframe unmounted while unavailable, and retries every second with cleanup. Any successful API status (`idle`, `building`, or `ready`) permits mount because iframe `/api/graph` is what starts a cold graph build.
- Jarvis `Graph3D` could set every node's `visibleFlags` to zero when React positions arrived before the RAF channel populated `displayedPositionsRef`; subsequent RAF frames respected those zero flags and kept every node at scale 0 until a shape/size change. Visibility is now computed independently of position availability, while matrix placement alone waits for a position.
- Verification: BC `tsc --noEmit` PASS and production Next build PASS (31/31). Jarvis targeted ESLint PASS, Vite production bundle PASS (346 modules), and focused Vitest PASS 26/26. Cold-start test began with Vite up/API down (`/api/graph/status` 500), opened the graph once, then started API; the same tab recovered automatically to 292 visible nodes with no second click and console errors 0. Hidden-mount regression started canvas at 0×0, then revealed it; canvas recovered to 1280×720 and the rendered screenshot was 207,257 bytes (>150,000 graph-visible threshold).
- Known pre-existing Jarvis checks: full `npm run build` still has four unrelated `force3d.worker.ts` callback typing errors; broad `npm test` mixes eight Playwright specs into Vitest and fails those suites, while the actual unit suite passes. Advisor: GLM 5/10, DeepSeek 3/10. Updated `nextjs-frontend` with the WebGL readiness/visibility lesson. No commit or push.

- Verification: graph API reports 292 nodes / 548 links / 17 collections / 274 types / exact tag counts 17 `mongodb` + 274 `general-type`; browser graph shows the two legends, `#mongodb` isolates `VISIBLE: 17`, and `#general-type` isolates `VISIBLE: 274`. Engine recovered in ~10s; containers auto-started (mainapi/mongodb/postgres/clickhouse/kafka/redis/minio all healthy).
